Instead of attempting to
dominate

the combinatorics of such formulas in any dimension

n\geqslant 2
, we will
infinitesimalize

the determination of the stability group at order

n+1
, and also, we will
infinitesimalize

its action on coefficients of order
n+2
.

16
.
Tangency at Order
2
in Dimension
n

Before starting,
in any dimension
n\geqslant 2
and in continuation
with Section
7
,
let us examine tangency of
L

up to order
2
to the
hypersurface
u=\frac{1}{2}\,x_{1}^{2}+{\rm O}_{x}(3)
.
Thus, in (
7
),
put
T_{1}:=0
, …,
T_{n}:=0
:

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=\,\,\,\,\,\,\Big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{1,n}\,x_{n}+B_{1}\,u\Big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\,+

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{2,n}\,x_{n}+B_{2}\,u\Big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial x_{2}}\,+

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot\cdot\,+

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}+B_{n}\,u\Big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial x_{n}}\,+

\displaystyle+\Big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +D\,u\Big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u}.

Lemma 16.1
.

Tangency
\pi^{2}\big(L(-u+F)\big|_{u=F}\big)

up to order
2
holds iff the coefficients matrix
of
L
reads:

\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&\cdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{1}\\
A_{2,1}&A_{2,2}&\cdots&A_{2,n}&B_{2}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots&\vdots\\
A_{n,1}&A_{n,2}&\cdots&A_{n,n}&B_{n}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&\cdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf 2}}.

Proof.

Write the graph as
u=\frac{1}{2}\,x_{1}^{2}+{\rm O}_{x}(3)
,
and compute modulo
{\rm O}_{x}(3)
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,D\,\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\,x_{1}^{2}+{\rm O}_{x}(3)\big)

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{1,n}\,x_{n}+B_{1}\,{\rm O}_{x}(2)\Big)\,\big(x_{1}+{\rm O}_{x}(2)\big)

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{2,n}\,x_{n}+B_{2}\,{\rm O}_{x}(2)\Big)\,\big(0+{\rm O}_{x}(2)\big)

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+A_{n,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}+B_{n}\,{\rm O}_{x}(2)\Big)\,\big(0+{\rm O}_{x}(2)\big).

The coefficients of
x_{1}^{2}
, of
x_{1}x_{2}
, …, of

x_{1}x_{n}
must vanish, which concludes:

D\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2\,A_{1,1}},\ \ \ \ \ A_{1,2}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0},\ \ \ \ \ \dots\dots,\ \ \ \ \ A_{1,n}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}.\qed

17
.
Tangency in Dimensions
n=2,3,4,5,6

Before endeavoring to treat the crucial order
n+1

in general dimension

n\geqslant 2
,
let us show what formulas exist in low dimensions.

\bullet

In dimension
n=2
, with:

\begin{aligned} u&\,=\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2},\end{aligned}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \begin{aligned} L&\,=\,\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+B_{1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{1}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}+B_{2}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{2}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(C_{1}\,x_{1}+C_{2}\,x_{2}+D\,u\big)\,\partial_{u},\end{aligned}

the tangency equation in orders
\leqslant 3
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,C_{1}\,x_{1}-C_{2}\,x_{2}-D\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+B_{1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big)\,\big(x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big),

gives at order
1
as we know:

C_{1}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ C_{2}\,:=\,0,

then at order
2
as we know:

D\,:=\,2\,A_{1,1},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{1,2}\,:=\,0,

and it remains:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-2\,A_{1,1}\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+B_{1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big)\,\big(x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle\equiv\,x_{1}^{3}\,\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\,B_{1}+\tfrac{1}{2}\,A_{2,1}\big]+x_{1}^{2}x_{2}\,\big[-\,A_{1,1}+A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{2}\,A_{2,2}\big],

which gives at order
3
:

B_{1}\,:=\,-\,A_{2,1},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{2,2}\,:=\,0.

The reductions of the coefficients matrix of
L
read:

\left[\begin{array}[]{ccc}A_{1,1}&A_{1,2}&B_{1}\\
A_{2,1}&A_{2,2}&B_{2}\\
C_{1}&C_{2}&D\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}0}}\leadsto\left[\begin{array}[]{ccc}A_{1,1}&A_{1,2}&B_{1}\\
A_{2,1}&A_{2,2}&B_{2}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&D\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}1}}\leadsto\left[\begin{array}[]{ccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{1}\\
A_{2,1}&A_{2,2}&B_{2}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}2}}\leadsto\left[\begin{array}[]{ccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{2}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&D\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}3}}.

\bullet

In dimension
n=3
, with:

\begin{aligned} u&\,=\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}}{2},\end{aligned}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \begin{aligned} L&\,=\,\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+A_{1,3}\,x_{3}+B_{1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{1}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}+A_{2,3}\,x_{3}+B_{2}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{2}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{3,1}\,x_{1}+A_{3,2}\,x_{2}+A_{3,3}\,x_{3}+B_{3}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{3}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(C_{1}\,x_{1}+C_{2}\,x_{2}+C_{3}\,x_{3}+D\,u\big)\,\partial_{u},\end{aligned}

the tangency equation up to order
4
,
whose expansion can be done,
is:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,C_{1}\,x_{1}-C_{2}\,x_{2}-C_{3}\,x_{3}-D\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}x_{1}+A_{1,2}x_{2}+A_{1,3}x_{3}+B_{1}\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{3}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}x_{1}+A_{2,2}x_{2}+A_{2,3}x_{3}+B_{2}\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+x_{1}^{2}x_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{3,1}x_{1}+A_{3,2}x_{2}+A_{3,3}x_{3}\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}}{6}\big).

Starting from order
2
thanks to
Lemma
16.1
,
the matrix reductions read:

\left[\begin{array}[]{cccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{1}\\
A_{2,1}&A_{2,2}&A_{2,3}&B_{2}\\
A_{3,1}&A_{3,2}&A_{3,3}&B_{3}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}2}}\,\,\,\leadsto\,\,\,\left[\begin{array}[]{cccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{2}\\
A_{3,1}&A_{3,2}&A_{3,3}&B_{3}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}3}}\,\,\,\leadsto\,\,\,\left[\begin{array}[]{cccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&B_{3}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}4}}.

\bullet

In dimension
n=4
, with:

\begin{aligned} u&\,=\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}}{2}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}x_{3}}{2},\end{aligned}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \begin{aligned} L&\,=\,\big(A_{1,1}x_{1}+A_{1,2}x_{2}+A_{1,3}x_{3}+A_{1,4}x_{4}+B_{1}u\big)\partial_{x_{1}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{2,1}x_{1}+A_{2,2}x_{2}+A_{2,3}x_{3}+A_{2,4}x_{4}+B_{2}u\big)\partial_{x_{2}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{3,1}x_{1}+A_{3,2}x_{2}+A_{3,3}x_{3}+A_{3,4}x_{4}+B_{3}u\big)\partial_{x_{3}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{4,1}x_{1}+A_{4,2}x_{2}+A_{4,3}x_{3}+A_{4,4}x_{4}+B_{4}u\big)\partial_{x_{4}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\big(C_{1}x_{1}+C_{2}x_{2}+C_{3}x_{3}+C_{4}x_{4}+Du\big)\partial_{u},\end{aligned}

one can convince oneself that up to order
4
included,
the same equations appear as in dimension
n=3
,
hence one can replace the coefficients
of
L
from what has been obtained just above, so that
the tangency equation up to order
5
reads:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,2\,A_{1,1}\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}-A_{2,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}\big]\big)\,\big(x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{4}}{6}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{3}\,A_{3,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+x_{1}^{2}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{3,1}\,x_{1}-A_{1,1}\,x_{3}+B_{3}\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{4,1}\,x_{1}+A_{4,2}\,x_{2}+A_{4,3}\,x_{3}+A_{4,4}\,x_{4}\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}}{24}\big).

By expanding this equation, one can see
that the matrix reduction at order

5
reads:

\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{3}\\
A_{4,1}&A_{4,2}&A_{4,3}&A_{4,4}&B_{4}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}4}}\,\,\,\leadsto\,\,\,\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&B_{4}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}5}}.

\bullet

In dimension
n=5
, with:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}x_{3}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{5}}{120}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{4}}{2}+x_{1}^{3}x_{2}^{2}x_{3}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{2}x_{4}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{3}^{2}}{8},

the tangency equation up to order
5
is:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,2\,A_{1,1}\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{5}}{120}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}-A_{2,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}\big]\big)\,\big(x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{4}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{5}}{24}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{3}\,A_{3,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+x_{1}^{2}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{6}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{3,1}\,x_{1}-A_{1,1}\,x_{3}-\tfrac{2}{4}\,A_{4,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{3}}{4}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{4,1}\,x_{1}-2\,A_{2,1}\,x_{3}-2\,A_{1,1}\,x_{4}+B_{4}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{2}}{6}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{5,1}\,x_{1}+A_{5,2}\,x_{2}+A_{5,3}\,x_{3}+A_{5,4}\,x_{4}+A_{5,5}\,x_{5}\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}}{120}\big),

and the matrix reduction is:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\left[\begin{array}[]{cccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{4}\\
A_{5,1}&A_{5,2}&A_{5,3}&A_{5,4}&A_{5,5}&B_{5}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}5}}\,\,\,\leadsto\,\,\,\left[\begin{array}[]{cccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{5}A_{5,1}}\\
A_{5,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{10}{3}A_{3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-5A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-3A_{1,1}}&B_{5}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}6}}.

\bullet

In dimension
n=6
, with:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}x_{3}}{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{5}}{120}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{4}}{2}+x_{1}^{3}x_{2}^{2}x_{3}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{2}x_{4}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{3}^{2}}{8}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{6}x_{6}}{720}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{5}}{2}+\tfrac{5}{3}x_{1}^{3}x_{2}^{3}x_{3}+\tfrac{5}{8}x_{1}^{4}x_{2}x_{3}^{2}+\tfrac{5}{12}x_{1}^{4}x_{2}^{2}x_{4}+\tfrac{1}{12}x_{1}^{5}x_{3}x_{4}+\tfrac{1}{24}x_{1}^{5}x_{2}x_{5},

the tangency equation up to order
6
is:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,2\,A_{1,1}\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{5}}{120}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{6}x_{6}}{720}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}-A_{2,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{5}}{120}\big]\big)\,\big(x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{4}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{5}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{6}}{120}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{3}\,A_{3,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{24}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+x_{1}^{2}x_{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{5}}{24}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{3,1}x_{1}-A_{1,1}x_{3}-\tfrac{2}{4}\,A_{4,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{6}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{3}}{4}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{4}}{12}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{4,1}\,x_{1}-2\,A_{2,1}\,x_{3}-2\,A_{1,1}\,x_{4}-\tfrac{2}{5}\,A_{5,1}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}}{24}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{2}}{6}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{3}}{12}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{5,1}\,x_{1}-\tfrac{10}{3}\,A_{3,1}\,x_{3}-5\,A_{2,1}\,x_{4}-3\,A_{1,1}\,x_{5}+B_{5}\,\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\big]\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}}{120}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{5}x_{2}}{24}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{6,1}\,x_{1}+A_{6,2}\,x_{2}+A_{6,3}\,x_{3}+A_{6,4}\,x_{4}+A_{6,5}\,x_{5}+A_{6,6}\,x_{6}\big)\,\big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{6}}{720}\big),

and the matrix reduction is:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\footnotesize\begin{aligned} \left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{5}A_{5,1}}\\
A_{5,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{10}{3}A_{3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-5A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-3A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{5}\\
A_{6,1}&A_{6,2}&A_{6,3}&A_{6,4}&A_{6,5}&A_{6,6}&B_{6}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}6}}\leadsto\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{5}A_{5,1}}\\
A_{5,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{10}{3}A_{3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-5A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-3A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{6}A_{6,1}}\\
A_{6,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-5A_{4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-10A_{3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-9A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-4A_{1,1}}&B_{6}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}7}}.\end{aligned}

18
.
Projections
\pi^{m}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
and

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})

For any integer
m\geqslant 1
and any power series vanishing
at the origin:

E(x)\,=\,E(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}\geqslant 0\atop\sigma_{1}+\cdots+\sigma_{n}\geqslant 1}\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\,E_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}},

define:

\pi^{m}\big(E(x)\big)\,:=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1}+\cdots+\sigma_{n}\leqslant m}\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\,E_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}.

Several times later, the following elementary fact will be useful.

Observation 18.1
.

Given two integers
k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{N}
and
two power series
H_{1}(x)\in{\rm O}_{x}(k_{1})
and

H_{2}(x)\in{\rm O}_{x}(k_{2})
, namely:

H_{1}(x)\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1}+\cdots+\sigma_{n}\geqslant k_{1}}\,H_{1,\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ H_{2}(x)\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1}+\cdots+\sigma_{n}\geqslant k_{2}}\,H_{2,\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}},

for any (homogeneous) order
m\geqslant 0
,
one has:

\pi^{m}\big(H_{1}\cdot H_{2}\big)\,=\,\pi^{m}\Big(\pi^{m-k_{2}}(H_{1})\cdot\pi^{m-k_{1}}(H_{2})\Big),

the right-hand side being understood as
0
when

m<{\sf min}\,(k_{1},k_{2})
.∎

As our hypersurfaces
H^{n}\subset\mathbb{R}^{n+1}
have constant
Hessian rank
1
, independent monomials are of interest.
Accordingly, define:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}\big(E(x)\big)\,:=\,\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\,\Big(E_{i+1,0,\dots,0}\,x_{1}^{i+1}+E_{i,1,\dots,0}\,x_{1}^{i}x_{2}+\cdots+E_{i,0,\dots,1}\,x_{1}^{i}x_{n}.\Big),

and also:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m}\,:=\,\pi^{m}\circ\pi_{{\sf ind}}\,=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}\circ\pi^{m},

that is:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m}\big(E(x)\big)\,:=\,\sum_{0\leqslant i\leqslant m-1}\,\Big(E_{i+1,0,\dots,0}\,x_{1}^{i+1}+E_{i,1,\dots,0}\,x_{1}^{i}x_{2}+\cdots+E_{i,0,\dots,1}\,x_{1}^{i}x_{n}.\Big).

Observation 18.2
.

Given two integers
k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{N}
and
two power series
H_{1}(x)\in{\rm O}_{x}(k_{1})
and

H_{2}(x)\in{\rm O}_{x}(k_{2})
,
for any (homogeneous) order
m\geqslant 0
,
one has:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m}\big(H_{1}\cdot H_{2}\big)\,=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m-k_{2}}(H_{1})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{m-k_{1}}(H_{2})\Big),

the right-hand side being understood as
0
when

m<{\sf min}\,(k_{1},k_{2})
.∎

Soon, we will need a notation to select
homogeneous

monomials
of order exactly equal to some fixed integeer
m\geqslant 1

(notice that the index
m

is lower-case now):

\pi_{m}\big(E(x)\big)\,:=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1}+\cdots+\sigma_{n}=m}\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\,E_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}},

and also to select the
independent
homogeneous ones:

\pi_{m}^{{\sf ind}}\big(E(x)\big)\,:=\,E_{m,0,\dots,0}\,x_{1}^{m}+E_{m-1,1,\dots,0}\,x_{1}^{m-1}x_{2}+\cdots+E_{m-1,0,\dots,1}\,x_{1}^{m-1}x_{n}.

19
.
Tangency at Order
n+1
in Dimension
n

Now, launch induction on dimension.
In any dimension
n-1
, in coordinates

(x_{1},\dots,x_{n-1},u)
,
we know that the hypersurface equation writes
up to order
n-1+1
as:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\sum_{m=3}^{n-2}\,\Big(\frac{x_{1}^{m}x_{m}}{m!}+x_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}+x_{1}^{n-2}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle+{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n-1}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x_{1},x_{2},\dots,x_{n-1}}(n+1),

with explicit independent and border-dependent monomials.

A linear (vanishing at the origin) affine vector field writes:

L\,=\,X_{1}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}+\cdots+X_{n-1}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n-1}}+U\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u},

with:

\displaystyle X_{1}

\displaystyle=\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+B_{1}\,u,

\displaystyle\cdots

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle X_{n-1}

\displaystyle=\,A_{n-1,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+B_{n-1}\,u,

\displaystyle U

\displaystyle=\,C_{1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+C_{n-1}\,x_{n-1}+D\,u.

We consider tangency of
L
to such a hypersurface

u=F(x_{1},\dots,x_{n-1})
up to order

n-1
, namely the condition:

0\,=\,\pi^{n-1}\Big(L\big(-u+F\big)\big|_{u=F}\Big),

which is equivalent to:

0\,=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-1}\Big(L\big(-u+F\big)\big|_{u=F}\Big).

We start considerations from
Lemma
16.1
at order
2
,
viewed in dimension
n-1
.

Induction Hypothesis 19.1
.

The vector space of fields
L
which are tangent to
order
\leqslant n-1
is of dimension
n-1+1
,
parametrized by
A_{1,1}
, …,
A_{n-1,1}
,
B_{n-1}
,
with the other constants defined by:

\underset{(1\leqslant i\leqslant n-1)}{A_{i,2}\,=\,0,}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \underset{(3\leqslant j\leqslant n-1)}{A_{1,j}\,=\,A_{2,j}\,=\,0,}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \underset{(3\leqslant j\leqslant i\leqslant n-1)}{A_{i,j}\,=\,{\textstyle{\frac{-(j-2)}{i-j+1}\,\binom{i}{j}}}\,A_{i-j+1,1},}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \underset{(3\leqslant j\leqslant n-2)}{B_{j}\,=\,-\tfrac{2}{j+1}\,A_{j+1,1},}

or equivalently, the matrix of the coefficients of
L
is:

\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{5}A_{4,1}}\\
\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\ddots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}\\
A_{n-2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-4}\binom{n-2}{3}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-5}\binom{n-2}{4}A_{n-5,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{n-1}A_{n-1,1}}\\
A_{n-1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-3}\binom{n-1}{3}A_{n-3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-4}\binom{n-1}{4}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-(n-3)A_{1,1}}&B_{n-1}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2\,A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right].

The goal is to show that similar expressions hold
in dimension
n
. Thus, in coordinates

(x_{1},\dots,x_{n-1},x_{n},u)
, consider a hypersurface
whose equation writes up to order
n+1
as:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\sum_{m=3}^{n-2}\,\Big(\frac{x_{1}^{m}x_{m}}{m!}+x_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}+x_{1}^{n-2}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

(19.2)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}+x_{1}^{n-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n-1},x_{n}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x_{1},x_{2},\dots,x_{n-1},x_{n}}(n+2),

and consider a linear vector field:

L\,=\,X_{1}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}+\cdots+X_{n-1}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n-1}}+X_{n}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n}}+U\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u},

with:

\displaystyle X_{1}

\displaystyle=\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+B_{1}\,u,

\displaystyle\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot\cdot

\displaystyle X_{n-1}

\displaystyle=\,A_{n-1,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n}\,x_{n}+B_{n-1}\,u,

\displaystyle X_{n}

\displaystyle=\,A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}+B_{n}\,u,

\displaystyle U

\displaystyle=\,C_{1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+C_{n}\,x_{n}+D\,u.

Similarly, we consider tangency of
L
to such a hypersurface
u=F(x_{1},\dots,x_{n-1},x_{n})
up to order
n
, namely the condition:

0\,=\,\pi^{n}\Big(L\big(-u+F\big)\big|_{u=F}\Big),

which is equivalent to:

\displaystyle 0\,=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\Big(L\big(-u+F\big)\big|_{u=F}\Big).

(19.3)

Putting
x_{n}:=0
, taking only
\pi_{\sf ind}^{n-1}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
,
applying the Induction
Hypothesis
19.1
,
we see that the matrix of coefficients of

L
incorporates known elements in dimension
n-1
:

\left[\begin{array}[]{cccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&A_{1,n}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&A_{2,n}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&A_{3,n}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&A_{4,n}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{5}A_{4,1}}\\
\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\ddots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}\\
A_{n-2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-4}\binom{n-2}{3}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-5}\binom{n-2}{4}A_{n-5,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&A_{n-2,n}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{n-1}A_{n-1,1}}\\
A_{n-1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-3}\binom{n-1}{3}A_{n-3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-4}\binom{n-1}{4}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-(n-3)A_{1,1}}&A_{n-1,n}&B_{n-1}\\
A_{n,1}&A_{n,2}&A_{n,3}&A_{n,4}&\cdots&A_{n-1,n}&A_{n,n}&B_{n}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&C_{n}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2\,A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right].

Without putting
x_{n}:=0
,
by examining
\pi_{\sf ind}^{n-1}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
, one can realize
that:

A_{1,n}\,=\,\cdots\,=\,A_{n-1,n}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ C_{n}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0},

and the detailed verification of these vanishings
is implicitly contained
in the proof of the next Lemma
19.4
.
So the matrix is:

\left[\begin{array}[]{cccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{5}A_{4,1}}\\
\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\ddots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}\\
A_{n-2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-4}\binom{n-2}{3}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-5}\binom{n-2}{4}A_{n-5,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{n-1}A_{n-1,1}}\\
A_{n-1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-3}\binom{n-1}{3}A_{n-3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-4}\binom{n-1}{4}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-(n-3)A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&B_{n-1}\\
A_{n,1}&A_{n,2}&A_{n,3}&A_{n,4}&\cdots&A_{n-1,n}&A_{n,n}&B_{n}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2\,A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right],

and in order to complete the induction,
we must determine the values of

A_{n,1}
,
A_{n,2}
,
A_{n,3}
,
A_{n,4}
,
…,
A_{n-1,n}
,
A_{n,n}
,
B_{n-1}
,
as follows.

Lemma 19.4
.

The tangency condition (
19.3
)
at order
n+1
forces the announced values:

B_{n-1}\,=\,-\,\frac{2}{n}\,A_{n,1},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{n,2}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \underset{(3\leqslant k\leqslant n)}{A_{n,k}\,=\,-\,\frac{k-2}{n-k+1}\,\binom{n}{k}\,A_{n-k+1,1}}.

Proof.

So, let us abbreviate the hypersurface equation (
19
)
including
independent and border-dependent monomials
up to order
n+1
as:

u\,=\,\overline{F}^{n+1}(x)+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+2),

and start to examine the tangency equation:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\,\equiv\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(L\big(-\,u+F\big)\big|_{u=F}\Big)

\displaystyle\,\equiv\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(-\,U(x,u)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,X_{i}(x,u)\cdot F_{x_{i}}(x)\Big|_{u=F(x)}\Big)

\displaystyle\,\equiv\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\bigg(-\,U\Big(x,\overline{F}^{n+2}(x)+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,X_{i}\Big(x,\overline{F}^{n+2}(x)+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+2)\Big)\cdot\Big(\overline{F}_{x_{i}}^{n+1}+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(1)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+1)\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\,\equiv\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\bigg(-\,U\big(x,\overline{F}^{n+2}(x)\big)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,X_{i}\big(x,\overline{F}^{n+2}(x)\big)\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{i}}^{n+1}(x)\bigg),

(19.5)

where
we use
0=\pi_{\sf ind}^{n+1}\big({\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(1)\big)=\pi^{n+1}\big({\rm O}_{x}(n+2)\big)
, and we use the fact that the

X_{i}\big(x,\overline{F}^{n+1}(x)\big)
are all
{\rm O}_{x}(1)
,
hence multiplied by the last remainder
{\rm O}_{x}(n+1)
become
{\rm O}_{x}(n+2)
.

In two steps,
we want to apply Observation
18.2

to the products

X_{i}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{i}}^{n+2}
above for
i=1,\dots,n
.
Firstly, each
X_{i}
is an
{\rm O}_{x}(1)
, so:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{i}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{i}}^{n+1}\big)\,=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}(X_{i})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{i}}^{n+1}\big)\Big).

Secondly, the vanishing orders at
0

of the
\overline{F}_{x_{i}}^{n+2}

increase, as one may verify, by
differentiating
19
,
that:

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{1}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{1}}{1!}\bigg|+\frac{x_{1}^{1}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n}}{(n-1)!},

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{2}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\bigg|+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{1!\,1!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{1!\,(n-2)!},

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{3}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{3}}{3!}\bigg|+\frac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}}{2!\,1!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-2}}{2!\,(n-3)!},

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n-3}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{n-3}}{(n-3)!}\bigg|+\frac{x_{1}^{n-3}x_{2}}{(n-4)!\,1!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{3}}{(n-4)!\,2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{4}}{(n-4)!\,3!},

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n-2}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\bigg|+\frac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{2}}{(n-3)!\,1!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{3}}{(n-3)!\,2!}

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n-1}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\bigg|+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{2}}{(n-2)!\,1!}

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{n}}{n!};

here for later use, the vertical bar separates the pure monomials

x_{1}^{\ast}

from the monomials
x_{1}^{\ast}x_{2},\dots,x_{1}^{\ast}x_{n}
.
Therefore, applying again
Observation
18.2
,
it suffice to know the independent parts of

X_{1}
,
X_{2}
,
X_{3}
, …,
X_{n-3}
,

X_{n-2}
,
X_{n-1}
,
X_{n}
up to decreasing orders:

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{1}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{1}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}(X_{1})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{1}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{2}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{2}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-1}(X_{2})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{2}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{3}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{3}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-2}(X_{3})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{3}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot\cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot\cdot

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{n-3}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{n-3}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{4}(X_{n-3})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n-3}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{n-2}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{n-2}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{3}(X_{n-2})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n-2}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{n-1}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{n-1}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{2}(X_{n-1})\cdot\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n-1}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(X_{n}\cdot\overline{F}_{x_{n}}^{n+1}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{1}(X_{n})\cdot\pi^{n}\big(\overline{F}_{x_{n}}^{n+1}\big)\Big),

Remind that in all the
X_{i}
, before taking

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-i+1}(X_{i})
as above,
we have to replace
u
by

\overline{F}^{n+1}(x)

which, for
3\leqslant i\leqslant n-2
, gives:

\displaystyle X_{i}

\displaystyle=\,X_{i}\big(x,\overline{F}^{n+1}(x)\big)

\displaystyle=\,A_{i,1}x_{1}-\sum_{3\leqslant j\leqslant i}\,\tfrac{j-2}{i-j+1}\,{\textstyle{\binom{i}{j}}}\,A_{i-j+1}\,x_{j}-\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,A_{i+1,1}\,\overline{F}^{n+2}(x),

with similar formulas for
i=1,2,n-1
.
Serendipitously, for all
3\leqslant j\leqslant i
,
the values of the coefficients of the
x_{j}

will be unimportant, hence we will abbreviate:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-i+1}(X_{i})\,=\,A_{i,1}\,x_{1}-\ast\,x_{3}-\cdots-\ast\,x_{i}-\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,A_{i+1,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-i}x_{n-i}}{(n-i)!}\Big].

Serendipitously also, in
X_{n}
,
no contribution of
B_{n}\,u\equiv B_{n}\,\overline{F}^{n+1}
occurs,
since

\overline{F}^{n+1}={\rm O}_{x}(2)
:

\displaystyle\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{1}(X_{n})

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{1}\Big(A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}+B_{n}\,{\rm O}_{x}(2)\Big)

\displaystyle=\,A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}.

At last, we can write in length all the terms
of the tangency equation (
19
),
as follows without mentionining

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\footnotesize\begin{aligned} 0&\,\equiv\,-\,2\,A_{1,1}\,\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{1,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\Big]\Big)\,\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{1}}{1!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-1}}{(n-2)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n}}{(n-1)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{2,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{1!\,1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{1!\,(n-3)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{1!\,(n-2)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{3,1}x_{1}-\ast x_{3}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-3}x_{n-3}}{(n-3)!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}}{3!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}}{2!\,1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-3}}{2!\,(n-4)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-2}}{2!\,(n-3)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{i,1}x_{1}-\ast x_{3}-\cdots-\ast x_{i}-\tfrac{2}{i+1}A_{i+1,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-i}x_{n-i}}{(n-i)!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{i}}{i!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{i}x_{2}}{(i-1)!1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-i}}{(i-1)!(n-i-1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-i+1}}{(i-1)!(n-i)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n-3,1}x_{1}-\ast x_{3}-\cdots-\ast x_{n-3}-\tfrac{2}{n-2}A_{n-2,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{3!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-3}}{(n-3)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-3}x_{2}}{(n-4)!1!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{3}}{(n-4)!2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{4}}{(n-4)!3!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n-2,1}x_{1}-\ast x_{3}-\cdots-\ast x_{n-2}-\tfrac{2}{n-1}A_{n-1,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{2}}{(n-3)!1!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{3}}{(n-3)!2!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n-1,1}x_{1}-\ast x_{3}-\cdots-\ast x_{n-1}+B_{n-1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{2}}{(n-2)!1!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n,1}x_{1}+\cdots+A_{n,k}x_{k}+\cdots+A_{n,n}x_{n}\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}}{n!}\Big).\end{aligned}

We know by the induction assumption that

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
applied to this gives zero.
Thus, when applying
\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
,
it suffices to collect all independent monomials of order

n+1
, namely
x_{1}^{n}x_{1}
,
x_{1}^{n}x_{2}
, …,

x_{1}^{n}x_{n}
, and to determine the coefficients
of these monomials, which should all vanish.

Let us explain how to determine the coefficient
of a general ‘intermediate’ monomial
x_{1}^{k}x_{k}
with

2\leqslant k\leqslant n-1
:

\bullet

the line
i=n-k
contributes
two
terms;

\bullet

the line
i=n-k+1
contributes
one
term;

\bullet

the line
i=k
contributes
one
term;

and that is all. Therefore, we obtain:

x_{1}^{k}x_{k}\,\Big(-\tfrac{2}{n-k+1}A_{n-k+1,1}\,\Big[\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-k-1)!(k-1)!}+\tfrac{1}{k!\,(n-k)!}\Big]+A_{n-k+1,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-k)!(k-1)!}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{n,k}\Big),

The coefficients of
x_{1}^{n}x_{1}
, of

x_{1}^{n}x_{2}
, can
be seen
/
determined directly from what is written,
and the coefficients of
x_{1}^{n}x_{n-1}
, of

x_{1}^{n}x_{n}
as well, so that we obtain:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,x_{1}^{n+1}\,\Big(\tfrac{1}{2!}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}\,B_{n-1}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{n,1}\Big)

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n}x_{2}\,\Big(\Big[\underline{-\tfrac{2}{n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-3)!1!}-\tfrac{2}{n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!(n-2)!}+\tfrac{1}{(n-2)!1!}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}\Big]A_{n-1,1}+\tfrac{1}{n!}A_{n,2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{k}x_{k}\,\Big(\Big[-\tfrac{2}{n-k+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-k+1)!(k-1)!}-\tfrac{2}{n-k+1}\,\tfrac{1}{k!(n-k)!}+\tfrac{1}{(n-k)!(k-1)!}\Big]A_{n-k+1,1}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{n,k}\Big)

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}\,\Big(\Big[-\tfrac{2}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{2!}\,\tfrac{1}{0!(n-2)!}-\tfrac{2}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!1!}+\tfrac{1}{1!(n-2)!}\Big]\,A_{2,1}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{n,n-1}\Big)

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n}x_{n}\,\Big(\Big[-2\,\tfrac{1}{n!}+\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}\Big]+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{n,n}\Big)

So, we obtain the announced values for

B_{n-1}
,
A_{n,2}
, …,
A_{n,k}
, …,
A_{n,n-1}
,

A_{n,n}
.
∎

In conclusion, the induction on the dimension
n
is complete.∎

20
.
Infinitesimal Action at Order
n+2

Beyond dimension
n\leqslant 6

(Section
15
),
we have no explicit
formula for the subgroup of
{\sf GL}(n+1,\mathbb{R})
which
stabilizes the normalization (
19
)
up to order
n+1
and which would be
valid for any
n\geqslant 2
.
We will therefore proceed in an infinitesimal manner.
This will be less expensive, computationally speaking.

Thanks to Section
19
, we may
take a vector field tangent
to (
19
)
up to order
\leqslant n+1
:

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle\,=\,\ \ \ \big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{1+1}\,A_{2,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{1}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{2+1}\,A_{3,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{2}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{3,1}\,x_{1}+0+A_{3,1}\,x_{3}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{3+1}\,A_{4,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{3}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

(20.1)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{n-1,1}x_{1}+0+A_{n-1,1}\,x_{3}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n-1}x_{n-1}\ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{n}\,A_{n,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{n-1}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\big(A_{n,1}x_{1}+0\ \ \ \ \ +A_{n,3}\,x_{3}+\cdots+A_{n,n-1}x_{n-1}+A_{n,n}x_{n}+B_{n}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{n}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2\,A_{1,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{u},

where, for all
3\leqslant j\leqslant i\leqslant n
:

\displaystyle A_{i,j}\,=\,-\,\frac{(j-2)}{i-j+1}\,\binom{i}{j}.

(20.2)

With small
\varepsilon\approx 0
, this
L
has the approximate flow:

\displaystyle y_{1}

\displaystyle\,=\,x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{1+1}\,A_{2,1}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle y_{2}

\displaystyle\,=\,x_{2}+\varepsilon\,\Big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{2+1}\,A_{3,1}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle y_{3}

\displaystyle\,=\,x_{3}+\varepsilon\,\Big(A_{3,1}\,x_{1}+0+A_{3,1}\,x_{3}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{3+1}\,A_{4,1}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle\cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

(20.3)

\displaystyle y_{n-1}

\displaystyle\,=\,x_{n-1}+\varepsilon\,\Big(A_{n-1,1}x_{1}+0+A_{n-1,1}\,x_{3}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n-1}x_{n-1}\ \ -\tfrac{2}{n}\,A_{n,1}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle y_{n}

\displaystyle\,=\,x_{n}+\varepsilon\,\Big(A_{n,1}x_{1}+0\ \ \ \ \ \ +A_{n,3}\,x_{3}+\cdots+A_{n,n-1}x_{n-1}+A_{n,n}x_{n}+B_{n}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\,=\,u\ \ +\varepsilon\,\Big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2\,A_{1,1}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

For
1\leqslant i\leqslant n-1
, we may write the intermediate
lines as:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ y_{i}

\displaystyle\,=\,x_{i}+\varepsilon\,\Big(A_{i,i}\,x_{1}+0+\sum_{3\leqslant j\leqslant i}\,A_{i,j}\,x_{j}+0+\cdots+0-\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,A_{i+1,1}\,u\Big)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

(20.4)

With its independent monomials of order
n+2
,
the hypersurface equation on the left is:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\sum_{m=3}^{n}\,\Big(\frac{x_{1}^{m}x_{m}}{m!}+x_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{1}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{(n+1)!}

(20.5)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +x_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(3)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+3).

Similarly,
the hypersurface equation on the right is:

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{y_{1}^{2}}{2}+\frac{y_{1}^{2}y_{2}}{2}+\sum_{m=3}^{n}\,\Big(\frac{y_{1}^{m}y_{m}}{m!}+y_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,\frac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{1}}{(n+2)!}+G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+G_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,\frac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{n}}{(n+1)!}

(20.6)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +y_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}+{\rm O}_{y^{\prime}}(3)+{\rm O}_{y}(n+3).

Now, assume that
(y_{1},\dots,y_{n},v)
are replaced
from (
20
)
in
0=-v+G(y)
, that

u
is replaced by
F(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})

from (
20
), and give a name
to the concerned fundamental equation (to be studied in a while):

0\,\equiv\,E(x_{1},\dots,x_{n},\varepsilon)\,=\,E(x,\varepsilon).

Consider terms of order
\leqslant n+1
:

\pi^{n+1}\bigg(\sum_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,E_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}(\varepsilon)\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\bigg)\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1}+\dots+\sigma_{n}\leqslant n+1}\,E_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}(\varepsilon)\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}.

Assertion 20.7
.

The fact that
L
is tangent up to order
n+1
implies:

0\,\equiv\,\pi^{n+1}\big(-v+G(y)\big)\,=\,\pi^{n+1}\,\big(E(x,\varepsilon)\big).

∎

Hence, look at order
n+2
terms, namely at:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,\pi^{n+2}\big(E(x,\varepsilon)\big)\,=\,\pi_{n+2}\big(E(x,\varepsilon)\big)

\displaystyle=:\,E_{n+2,0,\dots,0}(\varepsilon)\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{1}+E_{n+1,1,\dots,0}(\varepsilon)\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}+\cdots+E_{n+1,0,\dots,1}(\varepsilon)\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}.

For
\varepsilon=0
, the map is the identity, hence without
computation we know that:

0\,\equiv\,\sum_{\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}=n+2}\,\bigg(-\frac{F_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}}{\nu_{1}!\cdots\nu_{n}!}+\frac{G_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}}{\nu_{1}!\cdots\nu_{n}!}+\varepsilon\,T_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\bigg)+{\rm O}_{x_{1},\dots,x_{n}}(n+3),

and our key goal is to determine these terms
T_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}
that are of order
1
in
\varepsilon
—  more
precisely,
to compute the independent ones:

\displaystyle T_{n+2,0,\dots,0},\ \ \ \ \ T_{n+1,1,\dots,0},\ \ \ \ \ \dots,\ \ \ \ \ T_{n+1,0,\dots,1}.

(20.8)

Equivalently, we may write the fundamental equation
by specifying the dependent monomials as the
remainder
{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(2)
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\,\equiv\,E(x,\varepsilon)

\displaystyle\,\equiv\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{1}\bigg(-\frac{F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}}{(n+2)!}+\varepsilon\,T_{n+2,0,\dots,0}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\bigg)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}\bigg(-\frac{F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}+\varepsilon\,T_{n+1,1,\dots,0}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\bigg)

(20.9)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}\bigg(-\frac{F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{G_{n+1,0,\dots,1}}{(n+1)!}+\varepsilon\,T_{n+1,0,\dots,1}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\bigg)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x_{1},x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(n+3).

Introduce the operator:

\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})\,:=\,\frac{d}{d\varepsilon}\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}\Big(\pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})\Big)\,=\,\pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(\frac{d}{d\varepsilon}\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})\Big),

which selects what we want to compute:

\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(E(x,\varepsilon)\big)\,=\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{1}}{(n+2)!}\,T_{n+2,0,\dots,0}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}\,T_{n+1,1,\dots,0}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{(n+1)!}\,T_{n+1,0,\dots,1}.

Also, for some use about remainders, we will need
to consider all independent monomials of order
\leqslant n+2

(notice the placement
/
switch of indices):

\Pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+2}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})\,:=\,\frac{d}{d\varepsilon}\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}\Big(\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+2}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})\Big)\,=\,\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+2}\Big(\frac{d}{d\varepsilon}\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})\Big).

Thus in summary, we must apply
\Pi_{n+2}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})

to all (numerous) terms of (
20
).
We start by the remainders.

Lemma 20.10
.

One has:

\frac{d}{d\varepsilon}\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}\big({\rm O}_{y_{2},\dots,y_{n}}(3)\big)\,=\,{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(2).

Proof.

Take any monomial
y_{2}^{\nu_{2}}\cdots y_{n}^{\nu_{n}}

with
\nu_{2}+\cdots+\nu_{n}\geqslant 3
,
and abbreviate (
20
) as:

y_{i}\,=\,x_{i}+\varepsilon\,R_{i}(x)+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})

[formula]

whence:

\displaystyle\big(x_{2}+\varepsilon\,R_{2}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\big)^{\nu_{2}}

\displaystyle\cdots\big(x_{n}+\varepsilon\,R_{n}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\big)^{\nu_{n}}\,=\,

\displaystyle=\,\Big(x_{2}^{\nu_{2}}+\nu_{2}\,x_{2}^{\nu_{2}-1}\,\varepsilon\,R_{2}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)\cdots\Big(x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}+\nu_{n}\,x_{n}^{\nu_{n}-1}\,\varepsilon\,R_{n}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle=\,x_{2}^{\nu_{2}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\nu_{2}\,x_{2}^{\nu_{2}-1}R_{2}x_{3}^{\nu_{3}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}+\cdots+x_{2}^{\nu_{2}}\cdots x_{n-1}^{\nu_{n-1}}\nu_{n}x_{n}^{\nu_{n}-1}R_{n}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

and here
\nu_{2}-1+\nu_{3}+\cdots+\nu_{n}\geqslant 2
,
…,
\nu_{2}+\cdots+\nu_{n-1}+\nu_{n}-1\geqslant 2

as well.
∎

Thus, the remainder
{\rm O}_{y_{2},\dots,y_{n}}(3)

in (
20
) has contribution equal to
0

in
\Pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+2}(E)
.

Next, still in (
20
),
consider border-dependent monomials from the sum

\sum_{m=3}^{n}
, but only for
m\leqslant n-1
at first.

Lemma 20.11
.

For every
3\leqslant m\leqslant n-1

and for all
i,j\geqslant 2
with
i+j=m+1
,
one has:

0\,=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(y_{1}^{m-1}y_{i}y_{j}\big).

Proof.

To simplify (
20.4
)
after replacement of
u
by
F
,
abbreviate:

\alpha_{i}\,:=\,-\,\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,\tfrac{A_{i+1}}{(n+1-m)!},

and use
\cdots
to denote
monomials of order
\leqslant n-m+1
:

\displaystyle y_{i}

\displaystyle=\,x_{i}+\varepsilon\,\bigg\{A_{i,1}+\sum_{3\leqslant j\leqslant i}\,A_{i,j}\,x_{j}-\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,A_{i+1,1}\,\Big[\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1-m}x_{n+1-m}}{(n+1-m)!}+{\rm O}_{x}(n-m+3)\Big]\bigg\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\,x_{i}+\varepsilon\,\Big\{\cdots+\alpha_{i}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}+{\rm O}_{x}(n-m+3)\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

write the product:

\displaystyle y_{1}^{m-1}y_{i}y_{j}

\displaystyle=\,\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big\{\cdots+\alpha_{1}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}+{\rm O}_{x}(n-m+3)\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{m-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\bigg(x_{i}+\varepsilon\,\Big\{\cdots+\alpha_{i}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}+{\rm O}_{x}(n-m+3)\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\bigg)^{1}

\displaystyle\cdot\bigg(x_{j}+\varepsilon\,\Big\{\cdots+\alpha_{j}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}+{\rm O}_{x}(n-m+3)\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\bigg)^{1},

and expand:

\displaystyle\pi_{n+2}\big(y_{1}^{m-1}y_{i}y_{j}\big)\,=\,\underline{\pi_{n+2}\big(x_{1}^{m-1}x_{i}x_{j}\big)}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}+\varepsilon\,\Big\{

\displaystyle x_{1}^{m-2}\,\tbinom{m-1}{1}\,\alpha_{1}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}\,x_{i}\,x_{j}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{m-1}\,\alpha_{i}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}\,x_{j}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{m-1}\,x_{i}\,\alpha_{j}\,x_{1}^{n+1-m}\,x_{n+1-m}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

To conclude,
observe that
x_{i}x_{j}
are dependent monomials,
and since
n-m+1\geqslant 2
, observe that the
two monomials
x_{n+1-m}\,x_{j}
and

x_{n+1-m}\,x_{i}
are also dependent.
∎

It therefore remains to compute:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(-\,v

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{y_{1}^{2}y_{2}}{2!}+\frac{y_{1}^{3}y_{3}}{3!}+\cdots+\frac{y_{1}^{n-1}y_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{1}^{n}y_{n}}{n!}+y_{1}^{n-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle+G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,\frac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{1}}{(n+2)!}+G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+G_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,\frac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{n}}{(n+1)!}

\displaystyle+y_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\bigg).

Since
\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
is linear,
we can proceed termwise.

Compute the first term:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}(-v)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\frac{d}{d\varepsilon}\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}\Big(-u-\varepsilon\,2\,A_{1,1}\,u+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,\pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(-2\,A_{1,1}\,\Big[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{1}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{(n+1)!}+{\rm O}_{x_{1},\dots,x_{n}}(n+3)\Big]\bigg),

and obtain:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}(-v)

\displaystyle\,=\,-\,\frac{2}{(n+2)!}\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}-\frac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}-\cdots-

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ -\cdots-\frac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}.

(20.12)

Next, for
\kappa\in\mathbb{N}
, abbreviating:

\mathscr{R}^{\kappa}\,:=\,{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x_{1},x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(\kappa),

compute the second term:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\footnotesize\begin{aligned} \Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{2}}{2}\big)&\,=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{2}\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\Big\{A_{1,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(2)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+2)\Big]\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{2}\bigg)\\
&\,=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{2}\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\Big\{\cdots-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}+\mathscr{R}^{n+2}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{2}\bigg),\end{aligned}

expand the square, select the coefficient of
\varepsilon^{1}
,
select the (single) independent monomial, and obtain:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{2}}{2}\big)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,2\,x_{1}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}\,=\,-\,\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}.

(20.13)

Treat the third term:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{2}y_{2}}{2}\big)\,=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{2!}

\displaystyle\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{2}\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}+\mathscr{R}^{n+1}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot

\displaystyle\Big(x_{2}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{3}\,A_{3,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}+\mathscr{R}^{n+1}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)

that is:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{2}y_{2}}{2}\big)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,x_{1}^{2}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{3}\,A_{3,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\,=\,-\,\tfrac{2}{3}\,\tfrac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}\,A_{3,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n+1}.

(20.14)

Similarly:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{3}y_{3}}{3!}\big)\,=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{3!}

\displaystyle\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{2}\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{(n-2)!}+\mathscr{R}^{n}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{3}

\displaystyle\cdot

\displaystyle\Big(x_{3}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{4}\,A_{4,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{(n-2)!}+\mathscr{R}^{n}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)

that is:

\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{3}y_{3}}{3!}\big)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{3!}\,x_{1}^{3}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{4}\,A_{4,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\,=\,-\,\tfrac{2}{4}\,\tfrac{1}{3!\,(n-2)!}\,A_{4,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-2}.

Now, consider general
m
with
3\leqslant m\leqslant n-1
:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{m}y_{m}}{m!}\big)\,=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{m!}

\displaystyle\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{2}\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-m+1}x_{n-m+1}}{(n-m+1)!}+\mathscr{R}^{n-m+3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{m}

\displaystyle\cdot

\displaystyle\Big(x_{m}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{m+1}\,A_{m+1,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-m+1}x_{n-m+1}}{(n-m+1)!}+\mathscr{R}^{n-m+3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg),

that is:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{y_{1}^{m}y_{m}}{m!}\big)

\displaystyle\,=\,\tfrac{1}{m!}\,x_{1}^{m}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{m+1}\,A_{m+1,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-m+1}x_{n-m+1}}{(n-m+1)!}

\displaystyle\,=\,-\ \tfrac{2}{m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{m!(n-m+1)!}\,A_{m+1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-m+1}.

(20.15)

For
m=n
, the result is different, two monomials will be obtained:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{1}{n!}\,y_{1}^{n}y_{n}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{n!}\,\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{2}\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\mathscr{R}^{3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{n}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots+B_{n}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\mathscr{R}^{3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)^{n}

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{1}{n!}\,x_{1}^{n}\,B_{n}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,{\textstyle{\binom{n}{1}}}\,x_{1}^{n-1}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{2}\,A_{2,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\,x_{n},

that is:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(\tfrac{1}{n!}\,y_{1}^{n}y_{n}\big)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{n!\,2!}\,B_{n}\,x_{1}^{n+2}-\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!\,2!}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}\,x_{n}.

(20.16)

Next, with any
i,j\geqslant 2
satisfying
i+j=n+1
,
whence
i,j\leqslant n-1
:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(y_{1}^{n-1}\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\big)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{2}\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\mathscr{R}^{3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{i}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,A_{i+1,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\mathscr{R}^{3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{j}+\varepsilon\,\Big[\cdots-\tfrac{2}{j+1}\,A_{j+1,1}\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\mathscr{R}^{3}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,0

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,x_{1}^{n-1}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{i+1}\,A_{i+1,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\,x_{j}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,x_{1}^{n-1}\,\big(-\tfrac{2}{j+1}\,A_{j+1,1}\big)\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}\,x_{i},

that is:

\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(y_{1}^{n-1}\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\big)\,=\,-\,\tfrac{1}{2(i+1)}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,A_{i+1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2(j+1)}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,A_{j+1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i}.

In fact, we must perform a sum:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(y_{1}^{n-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,-\,\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{j=2}^{n-1}\,\frac{1}{n-j+2}\,\frac{1}{(n-j)!(j-1)!}\,A_{n-j+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}

\displaystyle-\,\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{i=2}^{n-1}\,\frac{1}{n-i+2}\,\frac{1}{(i-1)!(n-i)!}\,A_{n-i+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i}

and by exchanging
j\longleftrightarrow i
in the
first sum, the two sums are
equal
, whence:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(y_{1}^{n-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)\,=\,-\,\sum_{i=2}^{n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{n-i+2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(n-i)!}\,A_{n-i+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i}.

(20.17)

The next term:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}\Big)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}}{(n+2)!}\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n+2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}}{(n+2)!}\,{\textstyle{\binom{n+2}{1}}}\,x_{1}^{n+1}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}

has value:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}\Big)\,=\,\tfrac{G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}.

(20.18)

Then:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{2}}{(n+1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n+1}\bigg)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{2}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,x_{1}^{n+1}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+\tfrac{G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,x_{1}^{n}\,{\textstyle{\binom{n+1}{1}}}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}x_{2},

that is:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{2}}{(n+1)!}\Big)\,=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}+\tfrac{G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}}{n!}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}.

(20.19)

Next:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{3}}{(n+1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n+1}\bigg)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{3}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{3,1}\,x_{1}-A_{1,1}\,x_{3}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,\Big\{x_{1}^{n+1}\,A_{3,1}\,x_{1}-x_{1}^{n+1}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{3}+x_{1}^{n}\,{\textstyle{\binom{n+1}{1}}}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}x_{3}\Big\},

that is:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{3}}{(n+1)!}\Big)\,=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}}{(n+1)!}\,\Big\{A_{3,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}+\tfrac{n}{(n+1)!}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}\,x_{3}\Big\}.

(20.20)

Then for general
k
with
3\leqslant k\leqslant n
,
compute  —  in
0\cdots 1\cdots 0
, the
1
is at position

k-1
—  :

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 1}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{k}}{(n+1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n+1}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{k}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{k,1}\,x_{1}+\underset{3\leqslant j\leqslant k}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,A_{k,j}\,x_{j}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\,\bigg\{x_{1}^{n+1}\,\Big(A_{k,1}\,x_{1}+\underset{3\leqslant j\leqslant k}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,A_{k,j}\,x_{j}\Big)+x_{1}^{n}\,{\textstyle{\binom{n+1}{1}}}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}x_{k}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\,\bigg\{A_{k,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}+\underset{3\leqslant j\leqslant k-1}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,A_{k,j}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}+\big(A_{k,k}+(n+1)\,A_{1,1}\big)\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{k}\bigg\}.

Now, replace the
A_{k,j}
by their values
from (
20.2
), especially:

A_{k,k}\,=\,-\,(k-2)\,A_{1,1},

and obtain:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,\tfrac{y_{1}^{n+1}y_{k}}{(n+1)!}\Big)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}

\displaystyle-\underset{3\leqslant j\leqslant k-1}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,\tfrac{j-2}{k-j+1}\,\tbinom{k}{j}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k-j+1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{n-k+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{k}.

(20.21)

Finally, for any
i,j\geqslant 2
with
i+j=n+2
:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(y_{1}^{n}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(\Big(x_{1}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{n}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{i}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{i,1}\,x_{1}+\underset{3\leqslant k\leqslant i}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,A_{i,k}\,x_{k}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(x_{j}+\varepsilon\,\Big[A_{j,1}\,x_{1}+\underset{3\leqslant l\leqslant i}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,A_{j,l}\,x_{l}+\mathscr{R}^{2}\Big]+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\Big)^{1}\bigg),

that is:

\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(y_{1}^{n}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,A_{i,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}+\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\,A_{j,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i}.

In fact, we must perform a sum:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\bigg(y_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\,-\,\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{j=2}^{n}\,\frac{1}{(n-j+1)!(j-1)!}\,A_{n-j+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}

\displaystyle-\,\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\,\frac{1}{(i-1)!(n-i+1)!}\,A_{n-i+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i},

and by exchanging
j\longleftrightarrow i
in the
first sum, the two sums are
equal
, whence:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\Big(y_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\tfrac{y_{i}y_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)\,=\,-\,\sum_{i=2}^{n}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(n-i+1)!}\,A_{n-i+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i}.

(20.22)

Now that we have computed all terms
(
20
),
(
20.13
),
(
20.14
),
(
20
),
(

),
(

),
(

),
(

),
(

(

),
(
