),
we can sum them, namely:

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\Pi_{n+2}

\displaystyle\big(-v+G(y)\big)\,=\,

\displaystyle=\,-\,\tfrac{2}{(n+2)!}\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}-\cdots-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{n!}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}

\displaystyle-\,\tfrac{1}{3}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}\,A_{3,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-1}-\cdots-\tfrac{2}{m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{m!(n-m+1)!}\,A_{m+1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-m+1}-\cdots-\tfrac{2}{n}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!2!}\,A_{n,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{n!2}\,B_{n}\,x_{1}^{n+2}-\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!2}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}-\sum_{i=2}^{n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{n-i+2}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(n-i)!}\,A_{n+2-i,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,G_{n+1,1,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n}x_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leqslant k\leqslant n}\,\bigg\{\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k,1}\,x_{1}^{n+2}-\underset{3\leqslant j\leqslant k-1}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,\tfrac{j-2}{k-j+1}\,\tbinom{k}{j}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k-j+1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{j}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{n-k+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}k_{x}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{n}\,\tfrac{1}{(i-1)!(n-i+1)!}\,A_{n-i+2,1}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{i},

and collect the coefficients of the independent monomials:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,x_{1}^{n+2}\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+2)!}\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\underset{2\leqslant k\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots}\,A_{k,1}+\frac{1}{n!2}\,B_{n}\Big\}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\underbrace{-\tfrac{2}{n}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!2!}\,A_{n,1}-\tfrac{1}{n}\,\tfrac{1}{1!(n-2)!}\,A_{n,1}+\tfrac{1}{1!}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}\,A_{n,1}}_{=\,\,A_{n,1}\,\tfrac{1}{n!2!}\,\big[-2-(n-1)2+n\cdot 2\big]\,\,=\,\,A_{n,1}\,\tfrac{1}{n!2!}\,\big[\underline{-2+2}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}\big]\,\,=\,\,0}\Big\}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{3}\Big\{

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,01\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{(n-3+3)}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,01\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-2,1}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{A_{n-1,1}\big[-\tfrac{2}{n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-2)!3!}-\tfrac{1}{n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!(n-3)!}+\tfrac{1}{2!(n-2)!}\big]}_{=\,\,A_{n-1,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!3!}\,\big[-2-(n-2)3+(n-1)3\big]\,\,=\,\,A_{n-1,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!3!}\,\big[-2+3\big]}\Big\}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{4}\Big\{

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,001\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{n-4+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,001\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-3,1}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{A_{n-2,1}\big[-\tfrac{2}{n-2}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-3)!4!}-\tfrac{1}{n-2}\,\tfrac{1}{3!(n-4)!}+\tfrac{1}{3!(n-3)!}\big]}_{=\,\,A_{n-2,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-2)!4!}\,\big[-2-(n-3)4+(n-2)4\big]\,\,=\,\,A_{n-2,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-2)!4!}\,\big[-2+4\big]}\Big\}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{5}\Big\{

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0001\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{n-5+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0001\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-4,1}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{A_{n-3,1}\big[-\tfrac{2}{n-3}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-4)!5!}-\tfrac{1}{n-3}\,\tfrac{1}{4!(n-5)!}+\tfrac{1}{4!(n-4)!}\big]}_{=\,\,A_{n-3,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-3)!5!}\,\big[-2-(n-4)5+(n-3)5\big]\,\,=\,\,A_{n-3,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-3)!5!}\,\big[-2+5\big]}\Big\}

+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{k}\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{n-k+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}

\displaystyle+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-k+1,1}

\displaystyle+A_{n-k+2,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-k+2)!k!}\,\big[-2+k\big]\Big\}

+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-1}\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 10}\,A_{1,1}+

\displaystyle\tfrac{4}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 10}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}

\displaystyle+A_{3,1}\,\tfrac{1}{3!(n-1)!}\,\big[-2+n-1\big]\Big\}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}\Big\{

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 01}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 01}\,A_{1,1}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{A_{2,1}\,\big[-\tfrac{1}{n!}-\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!2!}-\emptyset+\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!1!}\big]}_{=\,\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!n!}\,\big[-2-n+2n\big]\,\,=\,\,A_{2,1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!n!}\,\big[n-2\big]}\Big\}.

With this expression, we have therefore
computed the quantities
T_{n+2,0,\dots,0}
,

T_{n+1,1,\dots,0}
,
T_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}
, …,
T_{n+1,0,\dots,1}

introduced in (
20.8
).

21
.
Normalizations of Order
(n+2)
Monomials

Now, coming back to (
20
),
thanks to these expressions of
T_{n+2,0,\dots,0}
,

T_{n+1,1,\dots,0}
,
T_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}
, …,

T_{n+1,0,\dots,1}
,
the coefficients of

x_{1}^{n+2}
, of
x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}
, of
x_{1}^{n+1}x_{3}
, …,
of
x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}
must vanish, hence we obtain
for the first three:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,\tfrac{1}{(n+2)!}\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}+\tfrac{1}{(n+2)!}\,G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\,\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+2)!}\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\underset{2\leqslant k\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k,1}+\tfrac{1}{n!\,2}\,B_{n}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,1,\dots,0},

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\,\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{n-3+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-2,1}+\tfrac{-2+3}{(n-1)!3!}\,A_{n-1,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

for general
k
with
3\leqslant k\leqslant n-1
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\,\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{n-k+3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-k+1,1}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-2+k}{(n-k+2)!k!}\,A_{n-k+2,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

and for the last two:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\,\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{4}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\tfrac{-2+n-1}{3!\,(n-1)!}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\,\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+1)!}\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{3}{(n+1)!}\,G_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{-2+n}{2!\,n!}\,A_{2,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

Now, we want to solve the
G_{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}}
in terms of the

F_{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}}
.
For some
\nu=(\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n})\in\mathbb{N}^{n}
,
assume given an equation with constant
\alpha
,
\beta
,

\Lambda_{\nu}
:

0\,\equiv\,-\,F_{\nu}+G_{\nu}+\varepsilon\,\big\{\alpha\,F_{\nu}+\beta\,G_{\nu}+\Lambda_{\nu}\big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

To determine the term
S_{\nu}
with:

G_{\nu}\overset{\text{\bf?}}{\,=\,}F_{\nu}+\varepsilon\,S_{\nu}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

replace and identify:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,\underline{-\,F_{\nu}+F_{\nu}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}+\varepsilon\,S_{\nu}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})+\varepsilon\,\Big\{\alpha\,F_{\nu}+\beta\,\big(G_{\nu}+\varepsilon\,S_{\nu}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})\big)+\Lambda_{\nu}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})

\displaystyle\equiv\,\varepsilon\,\big\{S_{\nu}+(\alpha+\beta)\,G_{\nu}+\Lambda_{\nu}\big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

whence:

S_{\nu}\,:=\,-\,(\alpha+\beta)\,G_{\nu}-\Lambda_{\nu}.

Same kinds of formulas exist for a linear system
involving several
F_{\nu}
,
G_{\nu}
, as above.

We therefore obtain:

\displaystyle G_{n+2,0,\dots,0}

\displaystyle=\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}-\varepsilon\,\Big\{n\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\underset{2\leqslant k\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,(n+2)\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k,1}+\tbinom{n+2}{2}\,B_{n}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle G_{n+1,1,\dots,0}

\displaystyle=\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}-\varepsilon\,\Big\{(n-1)\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2})

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0,1,,\dots,0}

\displaystyle=\,F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}-\varepsilon\,\Big\{(n-2)\,F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-2,1}+\tfrac{-2+3}{(n-1)!3!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{n-1,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

for general
k
with
3\leqslant k\leqslant n-1
:

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,=\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}-\varepsilon\,\Big\{(n-k+1)\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}

\displaystyle+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-k+1,1}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-2+k}{(n-k+2)!k!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{n-k+2,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

and:

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}

\displaystyle=\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}-\varepsilon\,\Big\{2\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\tfrac{-2+n-1}{3!(n-1)!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{3,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}

\displaystyle=\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}-\varepsilon\,\Big\{1\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{-2+n}{2!n!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{2,1}\Big\}+{\rm O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

Since it is more natural,
we start from the last equation.

Proposition 21.1
.

One can normalize on the right, and then on the left also:

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle G_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle G_{n+2,0,0,\dots,0}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+2,0,0,\dots,0}

\displaystyle:=\,0,

while the coefficient of
\frac{1}{(n+1)!}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}
is a relative invariant:

G_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\,\propto\,\,F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}.

Proof.

Indeed, the variable
A_{2,1}
is free to make the first
normalization, then the variable
A_{3,1}
is free to
make the second one, and so on
until
B_{n}
is free make the last normalization.
Notice that the system is triangular.
Two other views of these normalizations
will be given in the next two
Section
23

and
22
.
∎

22
.
Alternative, More Direct Normalizations at Order
(n+2)

As we know from
Section
19
,
the matrix of coefficients of a vector field

L
tangent up to order
n+1
is:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\scriptsize\begin{aligned} \left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}}\\
A_{2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}}\\
A_{3,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}}\\
A_{4,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\tfrac{2}{5}A_{4,1}}\\
\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\ddots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}\\
A_{n-2,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-4}\binom{n-2}{3}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-5}\binom{n-2}{4}A_{n-5,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-(n-4)}{1}\binom{n-2}{n-2}A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{n-1}A_{n-1,1}}\\
A_{n-1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-3}\binom{n-1}{3}A_{n-3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-4}\binom{n-1}{4}A_{n-4,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-(n-4)}{2}\binom{n-1}{n-2}A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-(n-3)}{1}\binom{n-1}{n-1}A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-\frac{2}{n}A_{n,1}}\\
A_{n,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-1}{n-2}\binom{n}{3}A_{n-2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-2}{n-3}\binom{n}{4}A_{n-3,1}}&\cdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-(n-4)}{3}\binom{n}{n-2}A_{3,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-(n-3)}{2}\binom{n}{n-1}A_{2,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\frac{-(n-2)}{1}\binom{n}{n}A_{1,1}}&B_{n}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2\,A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right],\end{aligned}

So with these coefficients,

0\equiv\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}\big(L\big(-u+F(x)\big)\big|_{u=F(x)}\big)
.

Then we apply the derivation
L({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
to
the hypersurface equation
20

written up to order
n+2
,
and we want to compute
\pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}\big(L\big(-u+F(x)\big)\big|_{u=F(x)}\big)
,
namely we want to compute

\pi_{n+2}^{{\sf ind}}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})
of:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\footnotesize\begin{aligned} 0&\,\equiv\,-\,2\,A_{1,1}\,\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}+F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{1}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{(n+1)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{1,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}\Big]\Big)\,\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{1}}{1!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n}}{(n-1)!}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}}{n!}+\cdots+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{2,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{1!\,1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{1!\,(n-2)!}+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{1!(n-1)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{3,1}x_{1}-\ast A_{1,1}x_{3}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}}{3!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{2}}{2!\,1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-2}}{2!\,(n-3)!}+F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n-1}}{2!(n-2)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{i,1}x_{1}-\ast A_{i-2,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\ast A_{2,1}x_{i-1}-(i-2)A_{1,1}x_{i}-\tfrac{2}{i+1}A_{i+1,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-i+1}x_{n-i+1}}{(n-i+1)!}\Big]\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{i}}{i!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{i}x_{2}}{(i-1)!1!}+\cdots+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-i+1}}{(i-1)!(n-i)!}+F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n-i+2}}{(i-1)!(n-i+1)!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n-3,1}x_{1}-\ast A_{n-5,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\ast A_{2,1}x_{n-4}-(n-5)A_{1,1}x_{n-3}-\tfrac{2}{n-2}A_{n-2,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{3!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{4!}\Big]\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-3}}{(n-3)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-3}x_{2}}{(n-4)!1!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{3}}{(n-4)!2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{4}}{(n-4)!3!}+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0,0,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{5}}{(n-4)!4!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n-2,1}x_{1}-\ast A_{n-4,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\ast A_{2,1}x_{n-3}-(n-4)A_{1,1}x_{n-2}-\tfrac{2}{n-1}A_{n-1,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{3!}\Big]\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{2}}{(n-3)!1!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{3}}{(n-3)!2!}+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{4}}{(n-3)!3!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n-1,1}x_{1}-\ast A_{n-3,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\ast A_{2,1}x_{n-2}-(n-3)A_{1,1}x_{n-1}-\tfrac{2}{n}A_{n,1}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}\Big]\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{2}}{(n-2)!1!}+F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{3}}{(n-2)!2!}\Big)\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\Big(A_{n,1}x_{1}-\ast A_{n-2,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\ast A_{2,1}x_{n-1}-(n-2)A_{1,1}x_{n}+B_{n}\Big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\Big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}}{n!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}}{(n-1)!1!}+F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}}{(n-1)!2!}\Big).\end{aligned}

By careful inspection of the products and after relevant
simplifications, we find exactly the same
factors (up to sign) of
\varepsilon^{1}

as in the equations preceding
Proposition
21.1
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}\Big\{n\,F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\underset{2\leqslant k\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,(n+2)\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k,1}+\tbinom{n+2}{2}\,B_{n}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}\Big\{(n-1)\,F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{3}}{(n+1)!}\Big\{(n-2)\,F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-2,1}+\tfrac{-2+3}{(n-1)!3!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{n-1,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{k}}{(n+1)!}\Big\{(n-k+1)\,F_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\cdots+\ast\,A_{n-k+1,1}+\tfrac{-2+k}{(n-k+2)!k!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{n-k+2,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-1}}{(n+1)!}\Big\{2\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\,A_{1,1}+\ast\,A_{2,1}+\tfrac{-2+n-1}{3!(n-1)!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{3,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-1}}{(n+1)!}\Big\{1\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\,A_{1,1}+\tfrac{-2+n}{2!n!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{2,1}\Big\}.

In conclusion, this computation is a
shortcut

to the longer computation done previously with infinitesimal

\varepsilon
.

23
.
Normalizations at Order
(n+2)
Via Jet Prolongations

As before, consider
u=u(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
as
a function of
(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
. The letter
F
will not be used.
Then to each partial
derivative
u_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
,
one can associate an independent coordinate (variable),
denoted similarly
u_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}
,
or sometimes more simply
u_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}
.
Background appears
e.g.

in
[
9
]
.

For each integer
\kappa\geqslant 0
,
introduce the
jet space of order
\kappa
,
namely
\mathbb{R}^{n+\binom{n+\kappa}{\kappa}}
equipped with the
coordinates:

\Big(x_{1},\dots,x_{n},u,\,\big(u_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}\big)_{1\leqslant\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}\leqslant\kappa}\Big).

An abbreviation will be used:

u^{(\kappa)}\,:=\,\big(u_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}\big)_{1\leqslant\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}\leqslant\kappa}.

Introduce also, for
i=1,\dots,n
,
the
total differentiation operators:

{\sf D}_{x_{i}}\,:=\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}+u_{x_{i}}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u}+\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\,\sum_{\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}=m}\,u_{x_{i}x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}},

which commute one with another.

Now, given a general vector field in the
(x,u)
-space:

L\,=\,\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,X_{i}(x,u)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}+U(x,u)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u},

its prolongation
[
9
]
to the infinite jet space:

L^{(\infty)}\,=\,L+\sum_{\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}\geqslant 1}\,U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}},

expresses how the (differentiated)
flow of
L
acts on higher order jets,
and its coefficients
U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}

are uniquely determined by the formulas:

U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\,:=\,{\sf D}_{x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}}\cdots{\sf D}_{x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}\Big(U-\underset{1\leqslant i\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,X_{i}\cdot u_{x_{i}}\Big)+\underset{1\leqslant i\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,X^{i}\cdot u_{x_{i}x_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\nu_{n}}}.

It is known that they depend on jet coordinates of order

\leqslant\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}
:

U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\,=\,U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\Big(x,u,\,u^{(\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n})}\Big).

Now, according to Section
19

(
see
also the matrix at the
beginning of
Section
22
),
the general vector field which stabilizes
the normalizations up order
n+1
,

i.e.
which is tangent up to order
n+1

to (
19
), writes:

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=\,\Big(A_{1,1}x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}u\Big)\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{2,1}x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}u\Big)\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{3,1}x_{1}-A_{1,1}x_{3}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}u\Big)\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{3}}

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot\cdot

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{n-1,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{1}{n-3}\tbinom{n-1}{3}A_{n-3,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\tfrac{n-3}{1}\tbinom{n-1}{n-1}A_{1,1}x_{n-1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{n}A_{n,1}u\Big)\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n-1}}

\displaystyle+\Big(A_{n,1}x_{1}-\tfrac{1}{n-2}\tbinom{n}{3}A_{n-2,1}x_{3}-\cdots-\tfrac{n-3}{2}\tbinom{n}{n-1}A_{2,1}x_{n-1}-\tfrac{n-2}{1}\tbinom{n}{n}A_{1,1}x_{n}+B_{n}u\Big)\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +2A_{1,1}u\Big)\frac{\partial}{\partial u}.

Then prolong it to the jet space of order
n+2
:

\displaystyle L^{(n+2)}\,=\,L+\sum_{\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}=1}\,U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\big(x,u,\,u^{(1)}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle+\sum_{\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}=n+1}\,U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\big(x,u,\,u^{(n+1)}\big)\frac{\partial}{\partial u_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n}=n+2}\,U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\big(x,u,\,u^{(n+2)}\big)\frac{\partial}{\partial u_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}}.

The following (admitted) statement can be established
in a general theoretical context.

Proposition 23.1
.

If
L
is tangent to (
20
) up to order

n+1
, then at the origin
(x,u)=(0,0)
, it holds:

0\,=\,U_{\nu_{1},\dots,\nu_{n}}\big(0,0,\,u^{(\nu_{1}+\cdots+\nu_{n})}\big)

[formula]

Furthermore, at order equal to
n+2
, considering only independent
jets, still at the origin, it can be shown
(in a general theoretical context) that one recovers
(up to sign and a change of notation)
the expressions appearing in
Section
22
.

Proposition 23.2
.

If
L
is tangent to (
20
) up to order

n+1
, then at the origin
(x,u)=(0,0)
, it holds:

\displaystyle U_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\big(0,0,u^{(n+2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\,-\,n\,u_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}-\underset{2\leqslant k\leqslant n}{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\sum$\,}}\,(n+2)\,u_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{k,1}-\tbinom{n+2}{2}\,B_{n},

\displaystyle U_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\big(0,0,u^{(n+2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\,-\,(n-1)\,u_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1},

\displaystyle U_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\big(0,0,u^{(n+2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\,-\,(n-2)\,u_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,A_{1,1}-\ast\,A_{2,1}-\cdots-\ast\,A_{n-2,1}-\tfrac{-2+3}{(n-1)!3!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{n-1,1}

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle U_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\big(0,0,u^{(n+2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\,-\,(n-k+1)\,u_{n+1,0\cdots 1\cdots 0}\,A_{1,1}-\ast\,A_{2,1}-\cdots-\ast\,A_{n-k+1,1}-\tfrac{-2+k}{(n-k+2)!k!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{n-k+2,1}

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle U_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\big(0,0,u^{(n+2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\,-\,2\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,1,0}\,A_{1,1}-\ast\,A_{2,1}-\tfrac{-2+n-1}{3!(n-1)!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{3,1}

\displaystyle U_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\big(0,0,u^{(n+2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\,-\,1\,F_{n+1,0,\dots,0,1}\,A_{1,1}-\tfrac{-2+n}{2!n!}\,(n+1)!\,A_{2,1}.\qed

Example 23.3
.

For instance, when
n=3
, the vector field from
Section
17
:

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=\,\big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{2}A_{2,1}\,u\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}

\displaystyle+\big(A_{2,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{3}A_{3,1}\,u\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial x_{2}}

\displaystyle+\big(A_{3,1}\,x_{1}-A_{1,1}\,x_{3}-\tfrac{2}{4}A_{4,1}\,u\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial x_{3}}

\displaystyle+\big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2\,A_{1,1}\,u\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u},

has prolongation of order
5
above the origin
(x,u)=(0,0)

given by:

\displaystyle L^{(5)}

\displaystyle=\,-\,\big(3\,u_{5,0,0}\,A_{1,1}+5\,u_{4,1,0}\,A_{2,1}+5\,u_{4,0,1}\,A_{3,1}+10\,B_{3}\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{5,0,0}}

\displaystyle-\,\big(2\,u_{4,1,0}\,A_{1,1}\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{4,1,0}}

\displaystyle-\big(u_{4,0,1}\,A_{1,1}+2\,A_{2,1}\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{4,0,1}}.

So with
n=3
,
in the space
\mathbb{R}^{3}\in(u_{5,0,0},u_{4,1,0},u_{4,0,1})

of pure
(n+2)
-jets, we have a linear space
of vector fields
parametrized by the
4
free coefficients:

A_{1,1},\,A_{2,1},\,A_{3,1},\,B_{3}.

This is in fact the Lie algebra of the action of
the stability group in orders
\leqslant n+1

acting on pure
n+2
order monomials.

Without computing the flows of these vector fields
(which would amount to recover
the formulas of Section
15
),
we can realize (once again) that the two normalizations:

F_{4,0,1}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{5,0,0}\,:=\,0,

are possible, as follows.

\bullet

By taking
A_{2,1}:=1
and the others zero, we have:

L^{(5)}\,=\,-\,\big(5\,u_{4,1,0}\,\big)\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{5,0,0}}-0\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{4,1,0}}-2\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{4,0,1}},

and the flow of the
constant vector field along the
u_{4,0,1}
-axis
clearly crosses the axis
\{u_{4,0,1}=0\}
.

\bullet

Assuming
u_{4,0,1}=0
, by taking
B_{3}:=1
and the others zero,
we have:

L^{(5)}\,=\,-\,10\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{5,0,0}}-0\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{4,1,0}}-0\,\tfrac{\partial}{\partial u_{4,0,1}},

a vector field that stabilizes
\{u_{4,0,1}=0\}
,
and whose flow crosses the axis
\{u_{5,0,0}=0\}
.∎

24
.
Normalizations of Order
(n+3)
Monomials

Thanks to Proposition
21.1
,
several order
n+2
independent monomials can be normalized to zero:

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+2,0,\dots,0}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+1,0,1,\dots,0}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \dots\dots,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ F_{n+1,0,\dots,1}\,:=\,0,

(24.1)

hence if we come back
to (
20
)
and let appear the next order
n+3
monomials  —  disregarding
as before the body-dependent ones  —, we have
the equation:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\sum_{m=3}^{n}\,\Big(\frac{x_{1}^{m}x_{m}}{m!}+x_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+0+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+0+\cdots+0+x_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{1}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}}{(n+2)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}x_{2}}{n!}+x_{1}^{n+1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+3}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(3)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+4),

where the border-dependent monomials
written in the last line can be
checked to include the supplementary monomial

F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}x_{2}}{n!}

by reasoning as in
Proposition
12.1
.

Next, we come back to the expression of a general affine
vector field
L
tangent up to order
n+1
.
Taking account of
the current normalization (
24.1
),
by looking at the equation at the end of
Section
22
,
we see that the tangency of
L
up to order
n+2

requires:

A_{2,1}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{3,1}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \dots\dots,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{n-1,1}\,:=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ B_{n}\,:=\,0,

hence coming back to (
20
)
we get the following vector field:

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=\,\ \ \ \big(A_{1,1}\,x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \big)\,\partial_{x_{1}}

\displaystyle+\big(0\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \big)\,\partial_{x_{2}}

\displaystyle+\big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -A_{1,1}\,x_{3}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \big)\,\partial_{x_{3}}

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle+\big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -(n-3)A_{1,1}x_{n-1}\ \ \ \ \ -\tfrac{2}{n}\,A_{n,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{x_{n-1}}

\displaystyle+\big(A_{n,1}x_{1}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -(n-2)A_{1,1}x_{n}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \big)\,\partial_{x_{n}}

\displaystyle+\big(\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2\,A_{1,1}\,u\big)\,\partial_{u},

whose flow, according to Assertion
20.7

(at the next order), stabilizes monomials of
order
\leqslant n+2
.

Now, to see the action on order
n+3
monomials, we
have to apply
\pi_{n+3}^{{\sf ind}}({}_{{}^{{}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet\!}}}})

to the tangency equation, which gives (without writing
terms which will not contribute):

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,2\,A_{1,1}\Big(\cdots+F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{1}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}}{(n+2)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{1,1}x_{1}\big)\Big(\cdots+F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{3}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+0

\displaystyle+\big(-A_{1,1}x_{3}\big)\Big(\cdots+F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+x_{1}^{n+1}\,\tfrac{x_{n}}{2!(n-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+\big(-2A_{1,1}x_{4}\big)\Big(\cdots+F_{n+2,0,0,1,\dots,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+x_{1}^{n+1}\,\tfrac{x_{n-1}}{3!(n-2)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+\big(-(n-4)A_{1,1}x_{n-2}\big)\Big(\cdots+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+x_{1}^{n+1}\tfrac{x_{5}}{(n-3)!4!}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(-(n-3)A_{1,1}x_{n-1}-\tfrac{2}{n}A_{n,1}\big[\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}\big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{3!}\big]\Big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{2}}{1!(n-2)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{3}}{2!(n-2)!}+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{4}}{3!(n-2)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+\big(A_{n,1}x_{1}-(n-2)A_{1,1}x_{n}\big)\Big(\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}}{n!}\Big|+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}}{1!(n-1)!}+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\tfrac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{3}}{2!(n-1)!}\Big).

We then collect all the independent monomials of order
n+3
:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\scriptsize\begin{aligned} 0&\equiv x_{1}^{n+3}\Big\{-\tfrac{2}{(n+3)!}F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+2)!}F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}A_{n,1}\Big\}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}\Big\{\tfrac{-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}\Big\{\tfrac{-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}-\tfrac{1}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}-\tfrac{2}{n}\tfrac{1}{3!(n-1)!}A_{n,1}-\tfrac{2}{n}\tfrac{1}{2!}\tfrac{1}{(n-2)!2!}A_{n,1}+\tfrac{1}{2!(n-1)!}A_{n,1}\Big\}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}\Big\{\tfrac{-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}F_{n+2,0,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}-\tfrac{2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\\
&\ \ \ \ \ x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n-2}\Big\{\tfrac{-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0,0}A_{1,1}-\tfrac{n-4}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n-1}\Big\{\tfrac{-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0}A_{1,1}-\tfrac{n-3}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}\Big\{\tfrac{-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+1)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}A_{1,1}-\tfrac{n-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}A_{1,1}\Big\}.\end{aligned}

After simplifications, this contracts as:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv x_{1}^{n+3}\Big\{\tfrac{n+1}{(n+3)!}F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}A_{n,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}\Big\{\tfrac{n}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}\Big\{\tfrac{n-1}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}+\tfrac{1}{3!n!}A_{n,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}\Big\{\tfrac{n-2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,0,1,\dots,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n-2}\Big\{\tfrac{4}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n-1}\Big\{\tfrac{3}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1,0}A_{1,1}\Big\}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}\Big\{\tfrac{2}{(n+2)!}F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}A_{1,1}\Big\}.

Thus, we see that we can normalize:

F_{n+2,0,1,\dots,0}\,:=\,0,

while all the other independent coefficients of order
n+3

are relative invariants.

Finally, to stabilize the obtained order
(n+3)
normalizations:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2}+\sum_{m=3}^{n}\,\Big(\frac{x_{1}^{m}x_{m}}{m!}+x_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+0+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+0+\cdots+0+x_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+3,0,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{1}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+0+F_{n+2,0,0,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}}{(n+2)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0,\dots,1}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+1,1,\dots,0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}x_{2}}{n!}+x_{1}^{n+1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+3}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}+{\rm O}_{x^{\prime}}(3)+{\rm O}_{x}(n+4),

we deduce that:

A_{n,1}\,:=\,0.

The isotropy Lie algebra is
1
-dimensional, represented
by the matrix:

\left[\begin{array}[]{cccccccc}A_{1,1}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-2A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}\\
\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\ddots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}&\vdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\vdots}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-(n-3)A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&\cdots&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}-(n-2)A_{1,1}}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\cdots}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\bf 0}&{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}2\,A_{1,1}}\end{array}\right].

25
.
Orders
(n+4)
and
(n+3)

The corresponding matrix Lie group:

\left[\!\begin{array}[]{cccccccc}a_{1,1}&0&0&0&\cdots&0&0&0\\
0&1&0&0&\cdots&0&0&0\\
0&0&\frac{1}{a_{1,1}}&0&\cdots&0&0&0\\
0&0&0&\frac{1}{a_{1,1}^{2}}&\cdots&0&0&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
0&0&0&0&\cdots&\frac{1}{a_{1,1}^{n-3}}&0&0\\
0&0&0&0&\cdots&0&\frac{1}{a_{1,1}^{n-2}}&0\\
0&0&0&0&\cdots&0&0&a_{1,1}^{2}\end{array}\!\right],

consists of the plain dilations:

y_{1}\,=\,a_{1,1}\,x_{1},\ \ \ \ \ \ y_{2}\,=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ y_{3}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{a_{1,1}}\,x_{3},\ \ \ \ \ \ \dots,\ \ \ \ \ \ y_{n}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{a_{1,1}^{n-2}}\,x_{n},\ \ \ \ \ \ v\,=\,a_{1,1}^{2}\,u.

Observation 25.1
.

All power series coefficients
F_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}

are relative invariants.

Proof.

So the possible remaining maps
fixing the origin which
send a hypersurface normalized as above:

u\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,x_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\,F_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}},

to a similarly normalized hypersurface:

v\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,y_{1}^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots y_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\,G_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}},

are only the dilations:

y_{1}\,=\,{\text{a}}\,x_{1},\ \ \ \ \ \ y_{2}\,=\,0,\ \ \ \ \ \ y_{3}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\text{a}}}\,x_{3},\ \ \ \ \ \ \dots,\ \ \ \ \ \ y_{n}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\text{a}}^{n-2}}\,x_{n},\ \ \ \ \ \ v\,=\,{\text{a}}^{2}\,u.

where we have abbreviated
a_{1,1}=:{\text{a}}
.

After replacement:

{\text{a}}^{2}\,u\,=\,\sum_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,\big({\text{a}}\,x_{1}\big)^{\sigma_{1}}\,(x_{2})^{\sigma_{2}}\,\big(\tfrac{1}{{\text{a}}}\,x_{3}\big)^{\sigma_{3}}\,\cdots\,\big(\tfrac{1}{{\text{a}}^{n-2}}\,x_{n}\big)^{\sigma_{n}}\,\,G_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}},

an identification gives:

F_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}\,=\,{\text{a}}^{-2}\,{\text{a}}^{\sigma_{1}}\,{\text{a}}^{-\sigma_{3}}\,\cdots\,{\text{a}}^{-(n-2)\sigma_{n}}\,\,G_{\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}}.

Thus, each couple of power series coefficients are nonzero multiple
one of the other. Thus, they vanish (or do not vanish),
simultaneously.
∎

Consequently, at the next two orders
n+4
and
n+5
,
we will not perform any further normalization,
since this would create some branching.

But we must determine the
independent and border-dependent monomials
of homogeneous degrees
n+4
and
n+5
.

Proceeding as in Proposition
12.1

to take account of the hypothesis that the
Hessian matrix is of constant rank
1
,
we obtain the following explicit expressions.
We skip presenting the details of computations.

Theorem 25.2
.

In any dimension
n\geqslant 2
, every local hypersurface
H^{n}\subset\mathbb{R}^{n+1}
having constant Hessian rank
1

which is not affinely equivalent to a product
of
\mathbb{R}^{m}

(1\leqslant m\leqslant n)

with a hypersurface
H^{n-m}\subset\mathbb{R}^{n-m+1}
can
be affinely normalized as:

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle=\,\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}\,x_{2}}{2}+\sum_{m=3}^{n}\,\Big(\frac{x_{1}^{m}\,x_{m}}{m!}+x_{1}^{m-1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=m+1}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}\,x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\Big)

\displaystyle+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+x_{1}^{n}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+2}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}\,x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}\,x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}\,x_{4}}{(n+2)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}\,x_{n}}{(n+2)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}\,x_{2}\,x_{2}}{n!}+x_{1}^{n+1}\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+3}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}\,x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!+F_{n+4,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+3,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}\,x_{2}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}\,x_{3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}\,x_{4}}{(n+3)!}+\cdots+F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}\,x_{n}}{(n+3)!}

\displaystyle+x_{1}^{n+2}\,\bigg[\frac{F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\,x_{2}\,x_{2}+\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{2!\,n!}\,x_{2}\,x_{3}+\frac{F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\,x_{2}\,x_{4}+\cdots+\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}\,x_{2}\,x_{n}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+4}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}\,x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\bigg]

\footnotesize\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} {}&+F_{n+5,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+5}}{(n+5)!}+F_{n+4,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}\,x_{2}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+4,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}\,x_{3}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+4,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}\,x_{4}}{(n+4)!}+\cdots+F_{n+4,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}\,x_{n}}{(n+4)!}\\
&+x_{1}^{n+3}\,\bigg[\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,10\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}-\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}}{2!\,(n+1)!}\Big)x_{2}x_{2}+\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}}{2!\,(n+1)!}\Big)x_{2}x_{3}+\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{3!\,n!}\Big)x_{2}x_{4}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\frac{F_{n+3,00010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}\,x_{2}x_{5}+\cdots+\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}\,x_{2}x_{n}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\frac{F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}}{2!\,(n+2)!}\,x_{3}x_{4}+\cdots+\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{2!\,(n+2)!}\,x_{3}x_{n}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\sum_{i,j\geqslant 2\atop i+j=n+5}\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,\frac{x_{i}\,x_{j}}{(i-1)!(j-1)!}\bigg]+{\rm O}_{x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(3)+{\rm O}_{x_{1},x_{2},\dots,x_{n}}(n+6).\end{aligned}

This explicit expression of the graphing function

F(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
is our new starting point.

26
.
Summary of the Proof of the Main
Theorem
1.6

Now, we take a general affine vector field which does not
necessarily vanish at the origin:

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=\,\Big(T_{0}+C_{1}\,x_{1}+C_{2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+C_{n-1}\,x_{n-1}+C_{n}\,x_{n}\Big)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial u}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{1}+A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{1,n}\,x_{n}\Big)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{2}+A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{2,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{2,n}\,x_{n}\Big)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{2}}

\displaystyle+\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{n-1}+A_{n-1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{n-1,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{n-1,n}\,x_{n}\Big)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n-1}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{n}+A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+A_{n,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}\Big)\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n}}.

Remind that if
L
is tangent to
the hypersurface
H=\big\{u=F(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})\big\}
,
then
T_{0}=0
, since

u=F={\rm O}_{x}(2)
.

Here, the parameters
T_{1},\dots,T_{n}
are
tightly related to the

infinitesimal transitivity
of the action, since
the value of
L
at the origin is:

L\big|_{0}\,=\,T_{1}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}+\cdots+T_{n}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n}},

since homogeneity requires that:

T_{0}H\,=\,{\sf Span}_{\mathbb{R}}\,\big\{L\big|_{0}\colon\,L|_{H}\,\,\text{tangent to}\,\,H\big\},

and since, due again to
F={\rm O}_{x}(2)
:

T_{0}H\,=\,{\sf Span}\,\Big\{\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}},\,\,\dots,\,\,\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{n}}\Big\}.

Observation 26.1
.

For (infinitesimal) affine homogeneity to hold,
the parameters
T_{1},\dots,T_{n}

should remain absolutely free in all computations.∎

Now, such a general affine vector field
L

is an infinitesimal affine symmetry of our
hypersurface
H=\big\{u=F(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})\big\}

graphed as in
Theorem
25.2
,
if and only if
L|_{H}
is tangent to
H
,
if and only if the following power series identity holds in

\mathbb{R}\{x_{1},\dots,x_{n}\}
:

0\,\equiv\,L\big(-\,u+F\big)\Big|_{u=F}.

We will in fact ‘only’ study independent monomials of
order
\leqslant n+4
in this fundamental equation,
namely we will examine
/
compute:

\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+4}\Big(L\big(-\,u+F\big)\Big|_{u=F}\Big).

Reminding  —
see
also below  —  that:

F_{x_{1}}\,=\,{\rm O}_{x}(1),\ \ \ \ \ \ F_{x_{2}}\,=\,{\rm O}_{x}(2),\ \ \ \ \ \ \cdots,\ \ \ \ \ \ F_{x_{n-1}}\,=\,{\rm O}_{x}(n-1),\ \ \ \ \ \ F_{x_{n}}\,=\,{\rm O}_{x}(n),

we may therefore start out by writing:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\equiv\,-\,C_{1}\,x_{1}-C_{2}\,x_{2}-\cdots-C_{n-1}\,x_{n-1}-C_{n}\,x_{n}-D\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+4}(F)]

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{0}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{1}+A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{1,n}\,x_{n}+B_{1}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+3}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{1}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{1}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{2}+A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{2,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{2,n}\,x_{n}+B_{2}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+2}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{2}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{3}+A_{3,1}\,x_{1}+A_{3,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{3,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{3,n}\,x_{n}+B_{3}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n+1}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{3}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{3}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{4}+A_{4,1}\,x_{1}+A_{4,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{4,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{4,n}\,x_{n}+B_{4}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{4}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{4}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{5}+A_{5,1}\,x_{1}+A_{5,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{5,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{5,n}\,x_{n}+B_{5}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-1}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{5}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{5}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{6}+A_{6,1}\,x_{1}+A_{6,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{6,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{6,n}\,x_{n}+B_{6}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{n-2}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{6}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{6}}

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

\displaystyle\cdots

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{n-1}+A_{n-1,1}x_{1}+A_{n-1,2}x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n-1}x_{n-1}+A_{n-1,n}x_{n}+B_{n-1}\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{5}(F)\big]\Big)F_{x_{n-1}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{n-1}}

\displaystyle+\Big(T_{n}+A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+A_{n,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}+B_{n}\,\big[\pi_{{\sf ind}}^{4}(F)\big]\Big)\,F_{x_{n}}.

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Lambda_{n}}

Notice on the right above that we attribute names to these
1+n

lines:

{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{0}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{1}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{2}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{3}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{4}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{5}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{6}},\ \ \ \ \ \dots,\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{n-1}},\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\Lambda_{n}}.

In the next Section
27
,
we will compute some of the coefficients of the
monomials in this large equation,
namely we will compute some coefficients:

E_{[\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}]}\,:=\,\big[x^{\sigma_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{\sigma_{n}}\big]\,\Big(L\big(-\,u+F\big)\Big|_{u=F}\Big)

[formula]

which are linear in

C_{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}}
,

D
,

T_{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}}
,

A_{{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}},{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}}}
,

B_{\scriptscriptstyle{\bullet}}
, and which should vanish
for
L
to really be tangent to
H
:

E_{[\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}]}\,=\,0.

Especially, we will compute:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}

\displaystyle:=\,E_{[n+2,0,\dots,0,1]}\,=\,0,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}

\displaystyle:=\,E_{[n+3,0,\dots,0,1]}\,=\,0.

But before proceeding to the (non-straightforward) computations,
let us summarize the key reason
why affinely homogeneous models do
not
exist
in dimension
n\geqslant 5
.
We use
\ast
to denote any unspecified
real number whose value does not matter.

Proposition 26.2
.

For a hypersurface
\{u=F(x)\}

normalized as in
Theorem
25.2
,
after taking account
of some of the
other equations
E_{[\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{n}]}=0
,
these two specific equations

{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}

become of the form:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}}{\,=\,}\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}-\frac{1}{12\,(n-3)\,n!}\,T_{4}+\frac{2}{(n+2)!}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,A_{1,1},

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}}{\,=\,}\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}+\ast\,T_{3}-\frac{1}{30\,(n-4)\,n!}\,T_{5}+\frac{3}{(n+3)!}\,F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,A_{1,1}.

Admitting temporarily this fact, we can easily conclude our main

inexistence
result.

Proof of Theorem
1.6
.

If the power series coefficient
F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}=0

would be zero, then the first equation:

0\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}}{\,=\,}\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}-\frac{1}{12\,(n-3)\,n!}\,T_{4},

would consist of a
nontrivial
linear dependence relation
between
T_{1},\dots,T_{n}
, contradicting infinitesimal transitivity.

So necessarily,
F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\neq 0
.

But then from equation
{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}
, we can solve the isotropy
parameter:

A_{1,1}\,=\,\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}+\ast\,T_{4},

that we replace in
{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}
, getting, whatever
the value of
F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}
is:

0\,=\,\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}+\ast\,T_{3}+\ast\,T_{4}-\frac{1}{30\,(n-4)\,n!}\,T_{5}.

But such an equation is also
always
a
nontrivial

linear dependence relation
between
T_{1},\dots,T_{n}
, contradicting again infinitesimal
transitivity!
∎

Observe that
n\geqslant 5
was used in this argumentation.

27
.
Tangency Equations at Orders
\leqslant n+4

So it remains ‘only’ to prove Proposition
26.2
.

Proof of Proposition
26.2
.

Here is
\Lambda_{0}
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{0}

\displaystyle=\,-\,C_{1}\,x_{1}-\cdots-C_{n}\,x_{n}

\displaystyle-\,D\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}}{(n+2)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}

\displaystyle+F_{n+4,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+3,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}x_{4}}{(n+3)!}

\displaystyle+\cdots+F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{n}}{(n+3)!}\bigg].

Next, we must write
\Lambda_{1}
, …,
\Lambda_{n}
,
which all involve products.
We will denote the products using the sign "
\cdot
".

Here is
\Lambda_{1}
:

\footnotesize\!\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} \Lambda_{1}&\,=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{1}&+A_{1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{1,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{1,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{1}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}}{(n+2)!}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\cdots+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&x_{1}+x_{1}x_{2}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{3}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n}}{(n-1)!}+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}}{n!}\\
&+F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{4}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{(n+1)!}\\
&+F_{n+4,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+3,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}}{(n+2)!}+\cdots+F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+2)!}\\
&+F_{n+5,0\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+4,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+4,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+4,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{4}}{(n+3)!}+\cdots+F_{n+4,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{n}}{(n+3)!}\end{aligned}\right).\end{aligned}

Here is
\Lambda_{2}
:

\footnotesize\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} \Lambda_{2}&\,=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{2}&+A_{2,1}\,x_{1}+A_{2,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{2,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{2,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{2}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\sum_{m=3}^{n}\,\frac{x_{1}^{m-1}x_{m-1}}{1!\,(m-2)!}\\
&+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{(n-1)!}\\
&+F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{2}{n!}\,x_{1}^{n+1}x_{2}\\
&+F_{n+3,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{2\,x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}\,+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}}{2!\,n!}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}}{(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{(n+1)!}\\
&+F_{n+4,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+1)!}+\Big(\frac{2\,F_{n+3,10\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}-\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\Big)x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}+\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}}{2!\,(n+1)!}\Big)\,x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{3!\,n!}\Big)\,x_{1}^{n+3}x_{4}\end{aligned}\right).\end{aligned}

Here is
\Lambda_{3}
:

\footnotesize\!\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} \Lambda_{3}&\,=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{3}&+A_{3,1}\,x_{1}+A_{3,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{3,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{3,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{3}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n}}{n!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{3}}{3!}+\sum_{m=4}^{n}\,\frac{x_{1}^{m-1}x_{m-2}}{2!\,(m-3!)}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n-1}}{2!\,(n-2)!}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n}}{2!\,(n-1)!}\\
&+F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{3}}{2!\,n!}\\
&+F_{n+4,010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}}{2!\,(n+1)!}\Big)x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{4}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+\cdots+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{n}}{2!\,(n+1)!}\end{aligned}\right).\end{aligned}

Here is
\Lambda_{4}
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{4}

\displaystyle=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{4}&+A_{4,1}\,x_{1}+A_{4,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{4,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{4,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{4}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{n-1}}{(n-1)!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{4}}{4!}+\sum_{m=5}^{n}\,\frac{x_{1}^{m-1}x_{m-3}}{3!\,(m-4)!}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n-2}}{3!\,(n-3)!}\\
&+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-1}}{3!\,(n-2)!}\\
&+F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}}{3!\,(n-1)!}\\
&+F_{n+4,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+\Big(\frac{F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{3!\,n!}\Big)x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}+F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{2!\,(n+1)!}\end{aligned}\right).

Here is
\Lambda_{5}
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{5}

\displaystyle=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{5}&+A_{5,1}\,x_{1}+A_{5,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{5,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{5,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{5}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-2}x_{n-2}}{(n-2)!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{5}}{5!}+\sum_{m=6}^{n}\,\frac{x_{1}^{m-1}x_{m-4}}{4!\,(m-5)!}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n-3}}{4!\,(n-4)!}\\
&+F_{n+2,00010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-2}}{4!\,(n-3)!}\\
&+F_{n+3,00010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,00010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n-1}}{4!\,(n-2)!}\\
&+F_{n+4,00010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+3,00010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,00010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{n}}{4!\,(n-1)!}\end{aligned}\right).

Here is
\Lambda_{6}
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{6}

\displaystyle=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{6}&+A_{6,1}\,x_{1}+A_{6,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{6,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{6,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{6}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\cdots+\frac{x_{1}^{n-3}x_{n-3}}{(n-3)!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{6}}{6!}+\sum_{m=7}^{n}\,\frac{x_{1}^{m-1}x_{m-5}}{5!\,(m-6)!}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{n-4}}{5!\,(n-5)!}\\
&+F_{n+2,000010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{n-3}}{5!\,(n-4)!}\\
&+F_{n+3,000010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,000010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n-2}}{5!\,(n-3)!}\\
&+F_{n+4,000010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+3,000010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,000010\cdots 0}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{n-1}}{5!\,(n-2)!}\end{aligned}\right).

Here is
\Lambda_{n-1}
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{n-1}

\displaystyle=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{n-1}&+A_{n-1,1}\,x_{1}+A_{n-1,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n-1,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{n-1,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{n-1}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{3!}+\frac{x_{1}^{4}x_{4}}{4!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}}{(n-1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n-1}x_{2}}{(n-2)!\,1!}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{3}}{(n-2)!\,2!}\\
&+F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{4}}{(n-2)!\,3!}\\
&+F_{n+3,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{5}}{(n-2)!\,4!}\\
&+F_{n+4,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+3,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{6}}{(n-2)!\,5!}\end{aligned}\right).

Here is
\Lambda_{n}
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{n}

\displaystyle=\,\left(\begin{aligned} T_{n}&+A_{n,1}\,x_{1}+A_{n,2}\,x_{2}+\cdots+A_{n,n-1}\,x_{n-1}+A_{n,n}\,x_{n}\\
&+B_{n}\,\bigg[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{2}x_{2}}{2!}+\frac{x_{1}^{3}x_{3}}{3!}\bigg]\end{aligned}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\begin{aligned} {}&\frac{x_{1}^{n}}{n!}\\
&+\frac{x_{1}^{n}x_{2}}{(n-1)!\,1!}\\
&+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}}{(n+2)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+1}x_{3}}{(n-1)!\,2!}\\
&+F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}}{(n+3)!}+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{2}}{(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+2}x_{4}}{(n-1)!\,3!}\\
&+F_{n+4,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+4}}{(n+4)!}+F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{2}}{(n+2)!}+F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{3}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+\frac{x_{1}^{n+3}x_{5}}{(n-1)!\,4!}\end{aligned}\right).

Now, by looking carefully at these products, we can
determine (chase) the coefficients of some relevant monomials.

Firstly, to get equation

{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}
, we extract the coefficient of the monomial

x_{1}^{n+2}x_{n}
. By inserting vertical bars,
we indicate from which
\Lambda_{0,1,\dots,n}

the written terms come from:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}}{\,=\,}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{0}}\!\!\!}-\,D\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{1}}\!\!\!}+T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}+A_{1,1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+A_{1,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{2}}\!\!\!}+T_{2}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+A_{2,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}+B_{2}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}+B_{2}\,\frac{1}{2!\,n!}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{3}}\!\!\!}+A_{3,1}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{4}}\!\!\!}+T_{4}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-1)!}+A_{4,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{5}}\!\!\!}+A_{5,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,00010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}{\displaystyle\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{6}}\!\!\!}+A_{6,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,000010\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{7}}\!\!\!}+\cdots{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{n-1}}\!\!\!}+A_{n-1,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}}{(n+2)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{n}}\!\!\!}+A_{n,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}.

Secondly, to get equation

{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}
, we extract the coefficient of the monomial

x_{1}^{n+3}x_{n}
:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} 0&\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}}{\,=\,}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{0}}\!\!\!}-\,D\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+3)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{1}}\!\!\!}+T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+4,0\cdots 01}}{(n+3)!}+A_{1,1}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}+A_{1,n}\,\frac{F_{n+4,0\cdots 0}}{(n+3)!}+B_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+B_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{2}}\!\!\!}+A_{2,1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+A_{2,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,10\cdots 0}}{(n+2)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{3}}\!\!\!}+T_{3}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+A_{3,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,010\cdots 0}}{(n+3)!}+B_{3}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}+B_{3}\,\frac{1}{3!\,n!}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{4}}\!\!\!}+A_{4,1}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-1)!}+A_{4,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0010\cdots 0}}{(n+3)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{5}}\!\!\!}+T_{5}\,\frac{1}{4!\,(n-1)!}+A_{5,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,00010\cdots 0}}{(n+3)!}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{6}}\!\!\!}+A_{6,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,000010\cdots 0}}{(n+3)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{7}}\!\!\!}+\cdots{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{n-1}}\!\!\!}+A_{n-1,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 010}}{(n+3)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{n}}\!\!\!}+A_{n,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+3)!}.\end{aligned}

We see that we must yet determine the parameters:

\displaystyle D,

\displaystyle A_{1,n},

\displaystyle B_{1},

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{2,1},

\displaystyle A_{2,n},

\displaystyle B_{2},

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{3,1},

\displaystyle A_{3,n},

\displaystyle B_{3},

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ A_{4,1},

\displaystyle\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle A_{n-2,n},

\displaystyle A_{n-1,n},

\displaystyle A_{n,n},

in terms of:

T_{1},\,\,\dots,\,\,T_{n},\,\,A_{1,1}.

From:

E_{[2,0,\dots,0]}\colon\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0\,=\,\tfrac{1}{2}\,T_{2}+A_{1,1}-\tfrac{1}{2}\,D,

we determine:

D\,=\,T_{2}+2\,A_{1,1}.

Next:

\displaystyle E_{[1,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,A_{1,n},

\displaystyle E_{[2,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{1}{2!}\,A_{2,n},

\displaystyle E_{[3,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{1}{3!}\,A_{3,n},

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \cdot

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdot

\displaystyle E_{[n-2,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,\tfrac{1}{(n-2)!}\,A_{n-2,n},

\displaystyle E_{[n-1,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}+\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}\,A_{n-1,n},

\displaystyle E_{[n,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,-\,D\,\tfrac{1}{n!}+A_{1,1}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}+T_{2}\,\tfrac{1}{(n-1)!}+\tfrac{1}{n!}\,A_{n,n},

Replacing the values so obtained of
D
,
and of
A_{1,n}=\cdots=A_{n-2,n}=0
,
we obtain:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}}{\,=\,}-\,\big(T_{2}+2\,A_{1,1}\big)\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}+T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}+A_{1,1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}

\displaystyle+T_{2}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+B_{2}\,\frac{n+1}{2!\,n!}

\displaystyle+A_{3,1}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}+T_{4}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-1)!}

\displaystyle+A_{n-1,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}}{(n+2)!}+A_{n,n}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!},

and:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}}{\,=\,}-\,\big(T_{2}+2\,A_{1,1}\big)\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+3)!}+T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+4,0\cdots 01}}{(n+3)!}+A_{1,1}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+2)!}+B_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{n+4}{2!\,(n+2)!}

\displaystyle+A_{2,1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+T_{3}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{2!\,(n+1)!}+B_{3}\,\frac{3\,n+2}{12\cdot n!}

\displaystyle+A_{4,1}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-1)!}+T_{5}\,\frac{1}{4!\,(n-1)!}

\displaystyle+A_{n-1,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 010}}{(n+3)!}+A_{n,n}\,\frac{F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}}{(n+3)!}.

To find the values of
B_{1}
,
B_{2}
,
B_{3}
,
and of
A_{2,1}
,
A_{3,1}
,
A_{4,1}
, we consider
by patiently chasing in
\Lambda_{0}
,

\Lambda_{1}
, …,
\Lambda_{n}
, the three equations:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} {}&E_{[n+1,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon&\ \ \ 0&\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{1}}\!\!}T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+B_{1}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}+B_{1}\,\frac{1}{n!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{2}}\!\!\!}+A_{2,1}\,\frac{1}{(n-1)!}+\underline{A_{2,n}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}\\
&&\ \ \ &\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{3}}\!\!\!}+T_{3}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!},\\
&E_{[n+1,0,\dots,0,1,0]}\colon&\ \ \ 0&\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{1}}\!\!}T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}}{(n+1)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{2}}\!\!}+\underline{A_{2,n-1}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}+B_{2}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-2)!}+B_{2}\,\frac{1}{(n-1)!\,2!}\\
&&\ \ \ &\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{3}}\!\!}+A_{3,1}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-2)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{4}}\!\!}+T_{4}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-2)!},\\
&E_{[n+1,0,\dots,0,1,0,0]}\colon&\ \ \ 0&\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{1}}\!\!}T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 0100}}{(n+1)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{2}}\!\!}+\underline{A_{2,n-2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.37,0,0}\circ}}\frac{F_{n+1,10\cdots 0}}{(n+1)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{3}}\!\!}+B_{3}\,\frac{1}{2!\,2!\,(n-3)!}+B_{3}\,\frac{1}{(n-2)!\,3!}\\
&&\ \ \ &\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{4}}\!\!}+A_{4,1}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-3)!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\,\Bigg|^{\Lambda_{5}}\!\!}+T_{5}\,\frac{1}{4!\,(n-3)!}.\end{aligned}

in which the three underlined parameters vanish thanks to:

\displaystyle E_{[2,0,\dots,0,1]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,A_{2,n}\,\tfrac{1}{2!},

\displaystyle E_{[2,0,\dots,0,1,0]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,A_{2,n-1}\,\tfrac{1}{2!},

\displaystyle E_{[2,0,\dots,0,1,0,0]}\colon

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 0

\displaystyle=\,A_{2,n-2}\,\tfrac{1}{2!}.

After simplification
/
contraction, these three equations are:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}}{(n+1)!}+T_{3}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}+A_{2,1}\,\frac{1}{(n-1)!}+B_{1}\,\frac{n+2}{2!\,n!},

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}}{(n+1)!}+T_{4}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-2)!}+A_{3,1}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-2)!}+B_{2}\,\frac{n}{2!\,(n-1)!},

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,\frac{F_{n+2,0\cdots 0100}}{(n+1)!}+T_{5}\,\frac{1}{4!\,(n-3)!}+A_{4,1}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-3)!}+B_{3}\,\frac{3\,n-4}{2!\,3!\,(n-2)!}.

The obtained
3+3
equations organize as
3
pairs,
and enable us to solve  —  skipping elementary
computations  —  firstly:

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{2}{(n+1)(n-2)}+T_{3}\,\frac{n}{3\,(n-2)},

\displaystyle A_{2,1}

\displaystyle=\,-\,T_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\frac{2}{(n+1)(n-2)}-T_{3}\,\frac{2}{3}\,\frac{n-1}{n-2},

secondly:

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{4}{(n-3)n(n+1)}+T_{4}\,\frac{1}{6}\,\frac{n-1}{n-3},

\displaystyle A_{3,1}

\displaystyle=\,-\,T_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 010}\,\frac{6}{(n-3)n(n+1)}-T_{4}\,\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{n-2}{n-3},

thirdly:

\displaystyle B_{3}

\displaystyle=\,T_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 0100}\,\frac{12}{n(n-4)(n^{2}-1)}+T_{5}\,\frac{1}{10}\,\frac{n-2}{n-4},

\displaystyle A_{4,1}

\displaystyle=\,-\,T_{1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 0100}\,\frac{24}{n(n-4)(n^{2}-1)}-T_{5}\,\frac{2}{5}\,\frac{n-3}{n-4}.

Replacing all these values, we receive

{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}
as:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\,\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf I}}{\,=\,}\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}+T_{4}\,\bigg(\frac{1}{6}\,\frac{n-1}{n-3}\,\frac{n+1}{2!\,n!}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{n-2}{n-3}\,\frac{1}{2!\,(n-1)!}+\frac{1}{3!\,(n-1)!}\bigg)

\displaystyle+A_{1,1}\,F_{n+2,0\cdots 01}\,\bigg(-\,\frac{2}{(n+2)!}+\frac{1}{(n+1)!}-\frac{n-2}{(n+2)!}\bigg),

which is exactly what Proposition
26.2

showed (exercise),
and we also receive

{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}
as:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\,\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0.37,0}\bf II}}{\,=\,}\ast\,T_{1}+\ast\,T_{2}+\ast\,T_{3}+T_{5}\,\bigg(\frac{1}{10}\,\frac{n-2}{n-4}\,\frac{3\,n+2}{12\cdot n!}-\frac{2}{5}\,\frac{n-3}{n-4}\,\frac{1}{3!\,(n-1)!}+\frac{1}{4!\,(n-1)!}\bigg)

\displaystyle+A_{1,1}\,F_{n+3,0\cdots 01}\,\bigg(-\,\frac{2}{(n+3)!}+\frac{1}{(n+2)!}-\frac{n-2}{(n+3)!}\bigg),

which is also exactly what Proposition
26.2

showed (exercise).
∎
